# ============================================================================
# CHAOS REDUX - EVENT 15: THE UTOPIA MANIFESTO
# ============================================================================
# Runtime kernel for conservative weighted prefire selection, the four-value
# Ledger, routes, one Necessary Ground case, stewardship, league state,
# formation proof, evolutions, paid growth, and idempotent cleanup.
# ============================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Conservative weighted prefire selection
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

utopia_manifesto_prepare_candidate_weight = {
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_weight = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_score.base }

	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_is_weak_industrially = yes }
		add_to_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_weight = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_score.weak_factory }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_is_small = yes }
		add_to_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_weight = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_score.small_state }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_has_low_research = yes }
		add_to_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_weight = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_score.low_research }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_has_weak_infrastructure = yes }
		add_to_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_weight = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_score.weak_infrastructure }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_capital_is_secure = yes }
		add_to_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_weight = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_score.secure_capital }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_subject = no }
		add_to_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_weight = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_score.independent }
	}
	else = {
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_weight = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_score.subject_penalty }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_faction = no }
		add_to_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_weight = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_score.no_faction }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_is_coastal_or_island = yes }
		add_to_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_weight = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_score.coastal_or_island }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_inland_island }
		add_to_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_weight = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_score.inland_island }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_has_live_migration_pressure = yes }
		add_to_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_weight = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_score.migration_pressure }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_has_live_housing_pressure = yes }
		add_to_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_weight = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_score.housing_pressure }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_has_live_trade_pressure = yes }
		add_to_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_weight = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_score.trade_pressure }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_defensive_war = yes }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_weight = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_score.defensive_war_penalty }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { num_occupied_states > constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_weight = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_score.occupation_penalty }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { num_subjects > constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_weight = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_score.subject_network_penalty }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_country = {
				is_major = yes
				has_guaranteed = PREV
			}
		}
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_weight = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_score.major_guarantor_penalty }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_recent_industrial_growth }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_weight = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_score.recent_industrial_growth_penalty }
	}

	round_temp_variable = utopia_manifesto_candidate_weight
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = utopia_manifesto_candidate_weight
		min = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_limits.ticket_minimum
		max = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_limits.ticket_maximum
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_add_current_country_to_candidate_pool = {
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_weight = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_limits.ticket_minimum }
	utopia_manifesto_prepare_candidate_weight = yes
	for_loop_effect = {
		start = constant:utopia_manifesto_narrative_count.zero
		end = utopia_manifesto_candidate_weight
		value = utopia_manifesto_candidate_ticket_index
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_class = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_class.human_generic } }
			add_to_temp_array = { utopia_manifesto_class_one_candidates = THIS }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_class = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_class.ai_generic } }
			add_to_temp_array = { utopia_manifesto_class_two_candidates = THIS }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_class = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_class.approved_registry } }
			add_to_temp_array = { utopia_manifesto_class_three_candidates = THIS }
		}
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_prepare_random_event_fire = {
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_prefire_candidate_class = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_class.none }
	clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_class_one_candidates
	clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_class_two_candidates
	clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_class_three_candidates

	every_country = {
		if = {
			limit = { utopia_manifesto_is_class_one_candidate = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_class = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_class.human_generic }
			utopia_manifesto_add_current_country_to_candidate_pool = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { utopia_manifesto_is_class_two_candidate = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_class = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_class.ai_generic }
			utopia_manifesto_add_current_country_to_candidate_pool = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { utopia_manifesto_is_class_three_candidate = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_class = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_class.approved_registry }
			utopia_manifesto_add_current_country_to_candidate_pool = yes
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_class_one_candidates^num > constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero } }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_prefire_candidate_class = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_class.human_generic }
		random_scope_in_array = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_class_one_candidates
			save_event_target_as = utopia_manifesto_prefire_actor
			save_global_event_target_as = utopia_manifesto_latest_actor
			utopia_manifesto_evaluate_prefire_evolutions = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_class_two_candidates^num > constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero } }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_prefire_candidate_class = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_class.ai_generic }
		random_scope_in_array = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_class_two_candidates
			save_event_target_as = utopia_manifesto_prefire_actor
			save_global_event_target_as = utopia_manifesto_latest_actor
			utopia_manifesto_evaluate_prefire_evolutions = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_class_three_candidates^num > constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero } }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_prefire_candidate_class = constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_class.approved_registry }
		random_scope_in_array = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_class_three_candidates
			save_event_target_as = utopia_manifesto_prefire_actor
			save_global_event_target_as = utopia_manifesto_latest_actor
			utopia_manifesto_evaluate_prefire_evolutions = yes
		}
	}

	clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_class_one_candidates
	clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_class_two_candidates
	clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_class_three_candidates
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Candidate diagnostics
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

utopia_manifesto_clear_candidate_unavailability_reason_flags = {
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_already_handled
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_terminal_identity
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_nonstandard_country
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_major
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_non_generic_tree
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_active_event_package
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_protected_package
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_mature_tree
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_civil_war
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_offensive_war
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_near_capitulation
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_insecure_capital
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_faction_leader
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_industrial_power
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_subject_empire
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_extensive_occupation
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_unsafe_subject
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_candidate_available
}

utopia_manifesto_refresh_candidate_unavailability_reason = {
	utopia_manifesto_clear_candidate_unavailability_reason_flags = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_accepted
				has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_rejected
				has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_focus_tree_loaded
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_already_handled
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_has_terminal_identity = yes }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_terminal_identity
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { uses_normal_civilian_systems = yes } }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_nonstandard_country
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_major = yes }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_major
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_has_active_event_package = yes }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_active_event_package
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_has_protected_package = yes }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_protected_package
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_has_mature_generic_tree = yes }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_mature_tree
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					has_focus_tree = generic_focus
					utopia_manifesto_candidate_has_approved_light_tree = yes
				}
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_non_generic_tree
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_civil_war = yes }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_civil_war
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_offensive_war = yes }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_offensive_war
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { surrender_progress < constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_thresholds.maximum_surrender_progress } }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_near_capitulation
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_capital_is_secure = yes } }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_insecure_capital
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_is_dominant_faction_leader = yes }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_faction_leader
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { num_of_factories > constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_limits.maximum_total_factories }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_industrial_power
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { num_subjects < constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_limits.extensive_subject_count } }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_subject_empire
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { num_occupied_states < constant:utopia_manifesto_candidate_limits.extensive_occupation_count } }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_extensive_occupation
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			is_subject = yes
			NOT = { utopia_manifesto_subject_candidate_is_safe = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unavailable_unsafe_subject
	}
	else = {
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_candidate_available
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Acceptance, rejection, and Ledger initialization
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

utopia_manifesto_accept_manifesto = {
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_manual_event_is_available = yes }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_accepted
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_rejected
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_kernel_disabled
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_ledger_visible
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_route = constant:utopia_manifesto_route.unresolved }
		utopia_manifesto_initialize_ledger = yes
		utopia_manifesto_initialize_callings = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_league_runtime = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_island_variant = yes
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_dynamic_costs = yes
		load_focus_tree = { tree = utopia_manifesto_tree keep_completed = no }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_focus_tree_loaded
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_recent_transformation_duration = constant:utopia_manifesto_durations.recent_transformation_days }
		set_country_flag = {
			flag = utopia_manifesto_transformation_recent
			days = utopia_manifesto_recent_transformation_duration
		}
		utopia_manifesto_consume_prefire_evolution_state = yes
		utopia_manifesto_apply_prefire_evolved_opening_packages = yes
		utopia_manifesto_evaluate_active_evolutions = yes
		recruit_character = utopia_manifesto_household_assembly
		recruit_character = utopia_manifesto_commonwealth_council
		recruit_character = utopia_manifesto_council_of_callings
		recruit_character = utopia_manifesto_rotating_congress
		recruit_character = utopia_manifesto_board_of_measure
		recruit_character = utopia_manifesto_college_of_measure
		recruit_character = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_council
		recruit_character = utopia_manifesto_directorate_of_service
		recruit_character = utopia_manifesto_interpreter
		recruit_character = utopia_manifesto_general_provisioner
		recruit_character = utopia_manifesto_secretary_of_callings
		recruit_character = utopia_manifesto_surveyor_of_shores
		recruit_character = utopia_manifesto_civic_engineer
		recruit_character = utopia_manifesto_keeper_of_stores
		recruit_character = utopia_manifesto_league_envoy
		recruit_character = utopia_manifesto_advocate_of_limits
		recruit_character = utopia_manifesto_public_auditor
		recruit_character = utopia_manifesto_constitutional_jurist
		recruit_character = utopia_manifesto_council_organizer
		recruit_character = utopia_manifesto_social_workshop_planner
		recruit_character = utopia_manifesto_chief_surveyor
		recruit_character = utopia_manifesto_standards_engineer
		recruit_character = utopia_manifesto_steward_of_service
		recruit_character = utopia_manifesto_contract_broker
		utopia_manifesto_initialize_identity_package = yes
		utopia_manifesto_initialize_achievement_lifecycle = yes
		utopia_manifesto_initialize_country_idea_lifecycles = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_prefire_public_circulation_delay = yes
		country_event = { id = chaosx.nr15.2 days = utopia_manifesto_event_delay }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_event_delay = mtth:utopia_manifesto_incident_interval }
		country_event = { id = chaosx.nr15.150 days = utopia_manifesto_event_delay }
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_reject_manifesto = {
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_rejected
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_accepted
	utopia_manifesto_clear_all_runtime_state = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_clear_ledger_live_breakdown = {
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_need_from_industry
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_plenty_from_industry
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_need_from_infrastructure
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_plenty_from_infrastructure
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_need_from_war
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_concord_from_war
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_need_from_occupation
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_concord_from_occupation
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_need_from_pressure
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_plenty_from_pressure
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_concord_from_pressure
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_need_from_capital
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_concord_from_capital
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_concord_from_institutions
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_assignment_from_institutions
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_need_from_subject_status
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_concord_from_subject_status
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_need_from_territorial_loss
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_concord_from_territorial_loss
}

utopia_manifesto_reset_ledger_live_breakdown = {
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_from_industry = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_plenty_from_industry = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_from_infrastructure = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_plenty_from_infrastructure = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_from_war = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_from_war = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_from_occupation = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_from_occupation = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_from_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_plenty_from_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_from_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_from_capital = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_from_capital = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_from_institutions = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_assignment_from_institutions = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_from_subject_status = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_from_subject_status = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_from_territorial_loss = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_from_territorial_loss = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
}

utopia_manifesto_clear_ledger_breakdown = {
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_need_from_base
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_plenty_from_base
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_concord_from_base
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_assignment_from_base
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_need_from_policy
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_plenty_from_policy
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_concord_from_policy
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_assignment_from_policy
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_need_last_delta
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_plenty_last_delta
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_concord_last_delta
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_assignment_last_delta
	utopia_manifesto_clear_ledger_live_breakdown = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_initialize_ledger = {
	utopia_manifesto_clear_ledger_breakdown = yes
	clear_variable = utopia_assignment_band
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_previous_assignment_band
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_balance_shift_to_choice_recent
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_balance_shift_to_assignment_recent
	utopia_manifesto_capture_ledger_acceptance_baseline = yes
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_ledger_visible
	set_variable = { utopia_reserve_score = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_defaults.reserve }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_from_base = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_defaults.need }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_plenty_from_base = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_defaults.plenty }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_from_base = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_defaults.concord }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_assignment_from_base = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_defaults.assignment }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_from_policy = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_plenty_from_policy = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_from_policy = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_assignment_from_policy = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_ledger = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_capture_ledger_acceptance_baseline = {
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_acceptance_owned_states
	every_owned_state = {
		limit = { is_core_of = ROOT }
		ROOT = { add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_acceptance_owned_states value = PREV } }
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_refresh_live_pressure_flags = {
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_migration_pressure
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_housing_pressure
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_trade_pressure
	if = { limit = { utopia_manifesto_has_live_migration_pressure = yes } set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_migration_pressure }
	if = { limit = { utopia_manifesto_has_live_housing_pressure = yes } set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_housing_pressure }
	if = { limit = { utopia_manifesto_has_live_trade_pressure = yes } set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_trade_pressure }
}

utopia_manifesto_recalculate_live_ledger_contributions = {
	utopia_manifesto_reset_ledger_live_breakdown = yes
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_is_weak_industrially = yes }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_from_industry = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_contribution.weak_industry_need }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_plenty_from_industry = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_contribution.weak_industry_plenty }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_from_industry = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_contribution.strong_industry_need }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_plenty_from_industry = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_contribution.strong_industry_plenty }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_has_weak_infrastructure = yes }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_from_infrastructure = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_contribution.poor_infrastructure_need }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_plenty_from_infrastructure = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_contribution.poor_infrastructure_plenty }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_war = yes }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_from_war = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_contribution.war_need }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_from_war = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_contribution.war_concord }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { num_occupied_states > constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_from_occupation = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_contribution.occupation_need }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_from_occupation = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_contribution.occupation_concord }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_capital_is_secure = yes }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_from_capital = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_contribution.secure_capital_need }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_from_capital = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_contribution.secure_capital_concord }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_government = democratic }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_from_institutions = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_contribution.institutional_concord }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_assignment_from_institutions = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_contribution.institutional_assignment }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_government = fascism
				has_government = communism
			}
		}
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_from_institutions = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_contribution.authoritarian_concord }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_assignment_from_institutions = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_contribution.authoritarian_assignment }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_subject = yes }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_from_subject_status = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_contribution.subject_need }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_from_subject_status = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_contribution.subject_concord }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_migration_pressure }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_from_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_contribution.migration_need }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_from_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_contribution.migration_concord }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_housing_pressure }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_from_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_contribution.housing_need }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_trade_pressure }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_from_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_contribution.trade_need }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_plenty_from_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_contribution.trade_plenty }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_territorial_state_loss = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_acceptance_owned_states
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_owned_by = ROOT } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_territorial_state_loss = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one }
		}
	}
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_from_territorial_loss = utopia_manifesto_territorial_state_loss }
	multiply_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_from_territorial_loss = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_contribution.territorial_loss_need_per_state }
	clamp_variable = {
		var = utopia_manifesto_need_from_territorial_loss
		min = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero
		max = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_contribution.territorial_loss_need_maximum
	}
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_from_territorial_loss = utopia_manifesto_territorial_state_loss }
	multiply_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_from_territorial_loss = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_contribution.territorial_loss_concord_per_state }
	clamp_variable = {
		var = utopia_manifesto_concord_from_territorial_loss
		min = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_contribution.territorial_loss_concord_minimum
		max = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_rebuild_ledger_totals = {
	set_variable = { utopia_need = utopia_manifesto_need_from_base }
	set_variable = { utopia_plenty = utopia_manifesto_plenty_from_base }
	set_variable = { utopia_concord = utopia_manifesto_concord_from_base }
	set_variable = { utopia_assignment = utopia_manifesto_assignment_from_base }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_need = utopia_manifesto_need_from_policy }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_plenty = utopia_manifesto_plenty_from_policy }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_concord = utopia_manifesto_concord_from_policy }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_assignment = utopia_manifesto_assignment_from_policy }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_need = utopia_manifesto_need_from_industry }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_plenty = utopia_manifesto_plenty_from_industry }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_need = utopia_manifesto_need_from_infrastructure }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_plenty = utopia_manifesto_plenty_from_infrastructure }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_need = utopia_manifesto_need_from_war }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_concord = utopia_manifesto_concord_from_war }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_need = utopia_manifesto_need_from_occupation }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_concord = utopia_manifesto_concord_from_occupation }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_need = utopia_manifesto_need_from_pressure }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_plenty = utopia_manifesto_plenty_from_pressure }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_concord = utopia_manifesto_concord_from_pressure }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_need = utopia_manifesto_need_from_capital }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_concord = utopia_manifesto_concord_from_capital }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_concord = utopia_manifesto_concord_from_institutions }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_assignment = utopia_manifesto_assignment_from_institutions }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_need = utopia_manifesto_need_from_subject_status }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_concord = utopia_manifesto_concord_from_subject_status }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_need = utopia_manifesto_need_from_territorial_loss }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_concord = utopia_manifesto_concord_from_territorial_loss }
}

utopia_manifesto_clamp_ledger_values = {
	clamp_variable = { var = utopia_need min = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_limits.minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_limits.maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = utopia_plenty min = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_limits.minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_limits.maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = utopia_concord min = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_limits.minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_limits.maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = utopia_assignment min = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_limits.minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_limits.maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = utopia_reserve_score min = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_limits.minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_limits.maximum }
}

utopia_manifesto_refresh_ledger_bands = {
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_need < constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_limits.band_two } } set_variable = { utopia_need_band = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_band.one } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_need < constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_limits.band_three } } set_variable = { utopia_need_band = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_band.two } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_need < constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_limits.band_four } } set_variable = { utopia_need_band = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_band.three } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_need < constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_limits.band_five } } set_variable = { utopia_need_band = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_band.four } }
	else = { set_variable = { utopia_need_band = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_band.five } }

	if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_plenty < constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_limits.band_two } } set_variable = { utopia_plenty_band = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_band.one } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_plenty < constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_limits.band_three } } set_variable = { utopia_plenty_band = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_band.two } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_plenty < constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_limits.band_four } } set_variable = { utopia_plenty_band = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_band.three } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_plenty < constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_limits.band_five } } set_variable = { utopia_plenty_band = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_band.four } }
	else = { set_variable = { utopia_plenty_band = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_band.five } }

	if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_concord < constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_limits.band_two } } set_variable = { utopia_concord_band = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_band.one } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_concord < constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_limits.band_three } } set_variable = { utopia_concord_band = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_band.two } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_concord < constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_limits.band_four } } set_variable = { utopia_concord_band = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_band.three } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_concord < constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_limits.band_five } } set_variable = { utopia_concord_band = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_band.four } }
	else = { set_variable = { utopia_concord_band = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_band.five } }

	if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_assignment < constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_limits.band_two } } set_variable = { utopia_assignment_band = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_band.one } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_assignment < constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_limits.band_three } } set_variable = { utopia_assignment_band = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_band.two } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_assignment < constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_limits.band_four } } set_variable = { utopia_assignment_band = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_band.three } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_assignment < constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_limits.band_five } } set_variable = { utopia_assignment_band = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_band.four } }
	else = { set_variable = { utopia_assignment_band = constant:utopia_manifesto_ledger_band.five } }
}

utopia_manifesto_refresh_balance_shift_animation = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = utopia_manifesto_previous_assignment_band
			utopia_manifesto_has_resolved_route = yes
			check_variable = {
				var = utopia_assignment_band
				value = utopia_manifesto_previous_assignment_band
				compare = less_than
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_balance_shift_to_assignment_recent
		set_temp_variable = {
			utopia_manifesto_balance_shift_animation_duration = constant:utopia_manifesto_durations.balance_shift_animation_days
		}
		set_country_flag = {
			flag = utopia_manifesto_balance_shift_to_choice_recent
			days = utopia_manifesto_balance_shift_animation_duration
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = utopia_manifesto_previous_assignment_band
			utopia_manifesto_has_resolved_route = yes
			check_variable = {
				var = utopia_assignment_band
				value = utopia_manifesto_previous_assignment_band
				compare = greater_than
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_balance_shift_to_choice_recent
		set_temp_variable = {
			utopia_manifesto_balance_shift_animation_duration = constant:utopia_manifesto_durations.balance_shift_animation_days
		}
		set_country_flag = {
			flag = utopia_manifesto_balance_shift_to_assignment_recent
			days = utopia_manifesto_balance_shift_animation_duration
		}
	}
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_previous_assignment_band
}

utopia_manifesto_refresh_reserve_band = {
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_reserve_score < constant:utopia_manifesto_reserve_band.band_two } } set_variable = { utopia_reserve_band = constant:utopia_manifesto_reserve_band.empty_stores } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_reserve_score < constant:utopia_manifesto_reserve_band.band_three } } set_variable = { utopia_reserve_band = constant:utopia_manifesto_reserve_band.emergency_stores } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_reserve_score < constant:utopia_manifesto_reserve_band.band_four } } set_variable = { utopia_reserve_band = constant:utopia_manifesto_reserve_band.seasonal_reserve } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_reserve_score < constant:utopia_manifesto_reserve_band.band_five } } set_variable = { utopia_reserve_band = constant:utopia_manifesto_reserve_band.one_year_security } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_reserve_score < constant:utopia_manifesto_reserve_band.band_six } } set_variable = { utopia_reserve_band = constant:utopia_manifesto_reserve_band.two_year_security } }
	else = { set_variable = { utopia_reserve_band = constant:utopia_manifesto_reserve_band.surplus_commonwealth } }
}

utopia_manifesto_refresh_ledger = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = utopia_assignment_band }
		set_variable = {
			utopia_manifesto_previous_assignment_band = utopia_assignment_band
		}
	}
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_live_pressure_flags = yes
	utopia_manifesto_recalculate_live_ledger_contributions = yes
	utopia_manifesto_rebuild_ledger_totals = yes
	utopia_manifesto_clamp_ledger_values = yes
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_ledger_bands = yes
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_balance_shift_animation = yes
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_reserve_band = yes
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_calling_state = yes
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_focus_visibility = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_ledger_delta = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_need_delta } }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_plenty_delta } }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_plenty_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_concord_delta } }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_assignment_delta } }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_assignment_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	}
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_last_delta = utopia_manifesto_need_delta }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_plenty_last_delta = utopia_manifesto_plenty_delta }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_last_delta = utopia_manifesto_concord_delta }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_assignment_last_delta = utopia_manifesto_assignment_delta }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_from_policy = utopia_manifesto_need_delta }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_plenty_from_policy = utopia_manifesto_plenty_delta }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_from_policy = utopia_manifesto_concord_delta }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_assignment_from_policy = utopia_manifesto_assignment_delta }
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_ledger = yes
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_plenty_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_assignment_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
}

utopia_manifesto_reset_calling_structural_pressure = {
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_structural_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_initial_pressure.base }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_structural_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_initial_pressure.base }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_structural_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_initial_pressure.base }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_structural_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_initial_pressure.base }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_structural_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_initial_pressure.base }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_structural_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_initial_pressure.base }
}

utopia_manifesto_seed_calling_pressure_from_country_conditions = {
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_need_is_high = yes }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_structural_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_initial_pressure.high_need }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_structural_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_initial_pressure.high_need }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_structural_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_initial_pressure.high_need }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { utopia_manifesto_reserve_is_secure = yes } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_structural_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_initial_pressure.reserve_gap }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_is_weak_industrially = yes }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_structural_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_initial_pressure.weak_industry }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_equipment = { support_equipment < constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_cost.support_medium } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_structural_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_initial_pressure.industrial_equipment_gap }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_has_weak_infrastructure = yes }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_structural_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_initial_pressure.weak_infrastructure }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_equipment = { train_equipment < constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_cost.trains_medium }
				has_equipment = { motorized_equipment < constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_cost.motorized_medium }
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_structural_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_initial_pressure.transport_equipment_gap }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { num_researched_technologies < constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_initial_pressure.learning_research_floor }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_structural_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_initial_pressure.low_research }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_migration_pressure }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_structural_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_initial_pressure.migration_pressure }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_structural_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_initial_pressure.migration_pressure }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_housing_pressure }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_structural_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_initial_pressure.housing_pressure }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_structural_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_initial_pressure.housing_pressure }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_trade_pressure }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_structural_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_initial_pressure.trade_pressure }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_structural_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_initial_pressure.trade_pressure }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_country_landlocked = yes }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_structural_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_initial_pressure.landlocked_network_gap }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_equipment = { convoy_1 < constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_cost.convoys_medium } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_structural_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_initial_pressure.coastal_convoy_gap }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_war = yes }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_structural_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_initial_pressure.war_pressure }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { num_occupied_states > constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_structural_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_initial_pressure.occupation_pressure }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_structural_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_initial_pressure.occupation_pressure }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_equipment = { infantry_equipment < constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_cost.infantry_medium }
				has_equipment = { support_equipment < constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_cost.support_medium }
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_structural_pressure = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_initial_pressure.defense_equipment_gap }
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_recalculate_temporary_calling_adjustments = {
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_temporary_adjustment = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_temporary_adjustment = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_temporary_adjustment = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_temporary_adjustment = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_temporary_adjustment = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_temporary_adjustment = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_emergency_levy_active }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_emergency_levy_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.provisioning } }
			set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_temporary_adjustment = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_resolution_change.emergency_levy }
			set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_method.emergency_levy }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_emergency_levy_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.workshops } }
			set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_temporary_adjustment = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_resolution_change.emergency_levy }
			set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_method.emergency_levy }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_emergency_levy_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.civic_works } }
			set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_temporary_adjustment = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_resolution_change.emergency_levy }
			set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_method.emergency_levy }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_emergency_levy_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.learning_and_care } }
			set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_temporary_adjustment = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_resolution_change.emergency_levy }
			set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_method.emergency_levy }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_emergency_levy_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.maritime_and_settlement } }
			set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_temporary_adjustment = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_resolution_change.emergency_levy }
			set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_method.emergency_levy }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_emergency_levy_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.defense_and_watches } }
			set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_temporary_adjustment = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_resolution_change.emergency_levy }
			set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_method.emergency_levy }
		}
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_rebuild_calling_severities = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_policy_adjustment } } set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_policy_adjustment = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_policy_adjustment } } set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_policy_adjustment = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_policy_adjustment } } set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_policy_adjustment = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_policy_adjustment } } set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_policy_adjustment = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_policy_adjustment } } set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_policy_adjustment = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_policy_adjustment } } set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_policy_adjustment = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero } }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_effective_policy_adjustment = utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_policy_adjustment }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_effective_policy_adjustment = utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_policy_adjustment }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_effective_policy_adjustment = utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_policy_adjustment }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_effective_policy_adjustment = utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_policy_adjustment }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_effective_policy_adjustment = utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_policy_adjustment }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_effective_policy_adjustment = utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_policy_adjustment }
	clamp_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_effective_policy_adjustment min = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.policy_adjustment_minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.policy_adjustment_maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_effective_policy_adjustment min = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.policy_adjustment_minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.policy_adjustment_maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_effective_policy_adjustment min = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.policy_adjustment_minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.policy_adjustment_maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_effective_policy_adjustment min = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.policy_adjustment_minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.policy_adjustment_maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_effective_policy_adjustment min = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.policy_adjustment_minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.policy_adjustment_maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_effective_policy_adjustment min = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.policy_adjustment_minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.policy_adjustment_maximum }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_uncommitted_severity = utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_structural_pressure }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_uncommitted_severity = utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_structural_pressure }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_uncommitted_severity = utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_structural_pressure }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_uncommitted_severity = utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_structural_pressure }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_uncommitted_severity = utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_structural_pressure }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_uncommitted_severity = utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_structural_pressure }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_uncommitted_severity = utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_effective_policy_adjustment }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_uncommitted_severity = utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_effective_policy_adjustment }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_uncommitted_severity = utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_effective_policy_adjustment }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_uncommitted_severity = utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_effective_policy_adjustment }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_uncommitted_severity = utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_effective_policy_adjustment }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_uncommitted_severity = utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_effective_policy_adjustment }
	clamp_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_uncommitted_severity min = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.severity_minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.severity_maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_uncommitted_severity min = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.severity_minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.severity_maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_uncommitted_severity min = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.severity_minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.severity_maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_uncommitted_severity min = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.severity_minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.severity_maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_uncommitted_severity min = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.severity_minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.severity_maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_uncommitted_severity min = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.severity_minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.severity_maximum }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_severity = utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_uncommitted_severity }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_severity = utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_uncommitted_severity }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_severity = utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_uncommitted_severity }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_severity = utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_uncommitted_severity }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_severity = utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_uncommitted_severity }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_severity = utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_uncommitted_severity }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_severity = utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_temporary_adjustment }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_severity = utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_temporary_adjustment }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_severity = utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_temporary_adjustment }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_severity = utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_temporary_adjustment }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_severity = utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_temporary_adjustment }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_severity = utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_temporary_adjustment }
}

utopia_manifesto_initialize_callings = {
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_policy_adjustment = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_policy_adjustment = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_policy_adjustment = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_policy_adjustment = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_policy_adjustment = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_policy_adjustment = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_temporary_adjustment = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_temporary_adjustment = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_temporary_adjustment = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_temporary_adjustment = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_temporary_adjustment = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_temporary_adjustment = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_method.unset }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_method.unset }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_method.unset }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_method.unset }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_method.unset }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_method.unset }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_emergency_levy_extensions = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_calling_state = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_refresh_calling_state = {
	utopia_manifesto_reset_calling_structural_pressure = yes
	utopia_manifesto_seed_calling_pressure_from_country_conditions = yes
	utopia_manifesto_recalculate_temporary_calling_adjustments = yes
	utopia_manifesto_rebuild_calling_severities = yes
	clamp_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_severity min = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.severity_minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.severity_maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_severity min = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.severity_minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.severity_maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_severity min = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.severity_minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.severity_maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_severity min = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.severity_minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.severity_maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_severity min = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.severity_minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.severity_maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_severity min = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.severity_minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.severity_maximum }
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_shortage }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_severity < constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.shortage_clear_threshold } } clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_shortage }
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_severity > constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.shortage_threshold } } set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_shortage }
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_shortage }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_severity < constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.shortage_clear_threshold } } clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_shortage }
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_severity > constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.shortage_threshold } } set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_shortage }
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_shortage }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_severity < constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.shortage_clear_threshold } } clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_shortage }
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_severity > constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.shortage_threshold } } set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_shortage }
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_shortage }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_severity < constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.shortage_clear_threshold } } clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_shortage }
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_severity > constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.shortage_threshold } } set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_shortage }
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_shortage }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_severity < constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.shortage_clear_threshold } } clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_shortage }
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_severity > constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.shortage_threshold } } set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_shortage }
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_shortage }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_severity < constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.shortage_clear_threshold } } clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_shortage }
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_severity > constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.shortage_threshold } } set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_shortage }
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_need_case_active }
		utopia_manifesto_validate_active_need_case = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_calling_change = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_calling_severity_delta } } set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_severity_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_calling_method_input } } set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_method_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_method.unset } }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_family_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.provisioning } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_policy_adjustment = utopia_manifesto_calling_severity_delta }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_method = utopia_manifesto_calling_method_input }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_family_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.workshops } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_policy_adjustment = utopia_manifesto_calling_severity_delta }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_method = utopia_manifesto_calling_method_input }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_family_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.civic_works } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_policy_adjustment = utopia_manifesto_calling_severity_delta }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_method = utopia_manifesto_calling_method_input }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_family_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.learning_and_care } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_policy_adjustment = utopia_manifesto_calling_severity_delta }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_method = utopia_manifesto_calling_method_input }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_family_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.maritime_and_settlement } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_policy_adjustment = utopia_manifesto_calling_severity_delta }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_method = utopia_manifesto_calling_method_input }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_family_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.defense_and_watches } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_policy_adjustment = utopia_manifesto_calling_severity_delta }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_method = utopia_manifesto_calling_method_input }
	}
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_calling_state = yes
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_family_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.none }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_severity_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_method_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_method.unset }
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Routes, decision phases, crisis, and geography
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

utopia_manifesto_refresh_focus_visibility = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_accepted
			NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_kernel_disabled }
		}
		mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_clear_route_flags = {
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_route_consent_of_households
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_route_common_table
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_route_guardians_of_measure
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_route_closed_island
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_route_joke_understood
}

utopia_manifesto_set_consent_of_households_route = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				utopia_manifesto_has_unresolved_route = yes
				utopia_manifesto_is_consent_of_households_route = yes
				has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_constitutional_crisis
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_penal_works_method_active } utopia_manifesto_end_penal_works_method = yes }
		utopia_manifesto_clear_route_flags = yes
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_route = constant:utopia_manifesto_route.consent_of_households }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_route_consent_of_households
		utopia_manifesto_consume_prepared_prefire_evolution_choice = yes
		utopia_manifesto_apply_prefire_route_resolved_effects = yes
		utopia_manifesto_evaluate_active_evolutions = yes
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_focus_visibility = yes
	}
}

	}

utopia_manifesto_set_decision_phase = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_decision_phase } }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_decision_phase = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_phase.locked }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_decision_phase_input } }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_decision_phase_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_phase.foundations }
	}
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = utopia_manifesto_decision_phase_input
		min = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_phase.locked
		max = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_phase.mature
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = utopia_manifesto_decision_phase_input
				value = utopia_manifesto_decision_phase
				compare = greater_than
			}
		}
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_decision_phase = utopia_manifesto_decision_phase_input }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_decision_phase_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_phase.foundations }
}

utopia_manifesto_unlock_calling_decisions = { set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_calling_decisions_unlocked }
utopia_manifesto_unlock_reserve_decisions = {
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_reserve_decisions_unlocked
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_prefire_project_deadlines = yes
}
utopia_manifesto_unlock_district_decisions = { set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_district_decisions_unlocked }
utopia_manifesto_unlock_necessary_ground_decisions = {
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_necessary_ground_decisions_unlocked
	utopia_manifesto_prepare_dominant_case_family = yes
	utopia_manifesto_prepare_case_candidate_targets = yes
}
utopia_manifesto_unlock_stewardship_decisions = { set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_decisions_unlocked }
utopia_manifesto_unlock_league_decisions = { set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_decisions_unlocked }
utopia_manifesto_unlock_formation_decisions = { set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_formation_decisions_unlocked }

utopia_manifesto_enter_constitutional_crisis = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_commonwealth_formed } }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_constitutional_crisis
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_constitutional_crisis_resolved
		country_event = { id = chaosx.nr15.7 }
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_focus_visibility = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_resolve_constitutional_crisis = {
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_constitutional_crisis
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_constitutional_crisis_resolved
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_focus_visibility = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_prepare_island_variant = {
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_opportunity_existing
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_opportunity_archipelago
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_opportunity_leased
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_opportunity_coastal
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_opportunity_inland
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_can_select_existing_island_variant = yes }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_opportunity_existing
	}
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_can_select_archipelago_network_variant = yes }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_opportunity_archipelago
	}
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_can_select_leased_island_variant = yes }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_opportunity_leased
	}
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_can_select_coastal_refuge_variant = yes }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_opportunity_coastal
	}
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_can_select_inland_island_variant = yes }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_opportunity_inland
	}
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_variant_surveyed
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_focus_visibility = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_clear_island_variant_choice_flags = {
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_geography_existing_island
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_geography_archipelago_network
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_geography_leased_island
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_geography_coastal_island
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_geography_inland_island
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_existing_island_plan
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_archipelago_network_plan
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_leased_island_plan
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_coastal_refuge_plan
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_inland_island_plan
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_leased_island_case_required
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_inland_island
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_geography_variant
}

utopia_manifesto_commit_island_variant = {
	utopia_manifesto_clear_island_variant_choice_flags = yes
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_geography_variant = utopia_manifesto_geography_variant_input }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_geography_variant = constant:utopia_manifesto_geography.existing_island } }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_geography_existing_island
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_existing_island_plan
		utopia_manifesto_register_capital_as_island_project_state = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_geography_variant = constant:utopia_manifesto_geography.archipelago_network } }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_geography_archipelago_network
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_archipelago_network_plan
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_archipelago_project_state_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_geography_variant = constant:utopia_manifesto_geography.leased_island } }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_geography_leased_island
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_leased_island_plan
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_leased_island_case_required
		utopia_manifesto_unlock_necessary_ground_decisions = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_geography_variant = constant:utopia_manifesto_geography.coastal_island } }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_geography_coastal_island
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_coastal_refuge_plan
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_district_visual_role = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_district_role.port_town }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_geography_variant = constant:utopia_manifesto_geography.inland_island } }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_geography_inland_island
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_inland_island_plan
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_inland_island
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_achievement_inland_island_path
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_district_visual_role = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_district_role.inland_island_ring }
		utopia_manifesto_register_capital_as_island_project_state = yes
	}
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_variant_selected
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_variant_choice_open
	utopia_manifesto_prepare_dominant_case_family = yes
	utopia_manifesto_prepare_case_candidate_targets = yes
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_focus_visibility = yes
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_formation_proof = yes
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_prefire_project_deadlines = yes
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_geography_variant_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_geography.unset }
}

utopia_manifesto_replan_island_variant = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_need_case_active
			check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_type = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_type.island_project_lease }
		}
		utopia_manifesto_renounce_active_need_case = yes
	}
	utopia_manifesto_clear_island_project_runtime = yes
	utopia_manifesto_commit_island_variant = yes
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_variant_replanned
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_plenty_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_nudge_loss }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_nudge_loss }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_assignment_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_nudge }
	utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_ledger_delta = yes
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Selected target and one active Necessary Ground case
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

utopia_manifesto_clear_domestic_review_state = {
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_domestic_alternative_survey_active
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_domestic_alternatives_reviewed
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_domestic_alternative_found
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_domestic_alternative_partial
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_no_domestic_alternative_found
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_domestic_review_external_case_authorized
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_domestic_review_solution_suppressed
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_domestic_review_no_solution_confirmed
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_domestic_alternative_insufficient
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_revised_for_alternative
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_domestic_review_family
}

utopia_manifesto_begin_domestic_alternatives_review = {
	utopia_manifesto_prepare_dominant_case_family = yes
	utopia_manifesto_clear_domestic_review_state = yes
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_domestic_review_family = utopia_manifesto_prepared_case_family }
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_domestic_alternative_survey_active
}

utopia_manifesto_invalidate_stale_domestic_review = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_domestic_alternative_survey_active }
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_domestic_alternatives_reviewed
			NOT = { utopia_manifesto_domestic_review_matches_prepared_family = yes }
		}
		utopia_manifesto_clear_domestic_review_state = yes
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_domestic_review_superseded
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_apply_domestic_review_relief = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_domestic_review_relief_input } }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_domestic_review_relief_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_domestic_review_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.provisioning } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_policy_adjustment = utopia_manifesto_domestic_review_relief_input }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_domestic_review_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.workshops } }
