		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_policy_adjustment = utopia_manifesto_domestic_review_relief_input }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_domestic_review_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.civic_works } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_policy_adjustment = utopia_manifesto_domestic_review_relief_input }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_domestic_review_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.learning_and_care } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_policy_adjustment = utopia_manifesto_domestic_review_relief_input }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_domestic_review_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.maritime_and_settlement } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_policy_adjustment = utopia_manifesto_domestic_review_relief_input }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_domestic_review_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.defense_and_watches } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_policy_adjustment = utopia_manifesto_domestic_review_relief_input }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_domestic_review_relief_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_calling_state = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_prepare_dominant_case_family = {
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_prepared_case_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.none }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family_context = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.none }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_dominant_case_score = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.severity_minimum }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_uncommitted_severity value = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.shortage_threshold compare = greater_than } }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_prepared_case_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.provisioning }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family_context = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.provisioning }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_dominant_case_score = utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_uncommitted_severity }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_uncommitted_severity value = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.shortage_threshold compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_uncommitted_severity value = utopia_manifesto_dominant_case_score compare = greater_than }
		}
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_prepared_case_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.workshops }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family_context = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.workshops }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_dominant_case_score = utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_uncommitted_severity }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_uncommitted_severity value = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.shortage_threshold compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_uncommitted_severity value = utopia_manifesto_dominant_case_score compare = greater_than }
		}
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_prepared_case_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.civic_works }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family_context = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.civic_works }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_dominant_case_score = utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_uncommitted_severity }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_uncommitted_severity value = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.shortage_threshold compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_uncommitted_severity value = utopia_manifesto_dominant_case_score compare = greater_than }
		}
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_prepared_case_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.learning_and_care }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family_context = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.learning_and_care }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_dominant_case_score = utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_uncommitted_severity }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_uncommitted_severity value = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.shortage_threshold compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_uncommitted_severity value = utopia_manifesto_dominant_case_score compare = greater_than }
		}
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_prepared_case_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.maritime_and_settlement }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family_context = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.maritime_and_settlement }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_dominant_case_score = utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_uncommitted_severity }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_uncommitted_severity value = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.shortage_threshold compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_uncommitted_severity value = utopia_manifesto_dominant_case_score compare = greater_than }
		}
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_prepared_case_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.defense_and_watches }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family_context = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.defense_and_watches }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_dominant_case_score = utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_uncommitted_severity }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_leased_island_case_required
			check_variable = {
				var = utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_uncommitted_severity
				value = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_limits.shortage_threshold
				compare = greater_than
			}
		}
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_prepared_case_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.maritime_and_settlement }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family_context = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.maritime_and_settlement }
	}
	utopia_manifesto_invalidate_stale_domestic_review = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_need_case_active
			has_variable = utopia_manifesto_case_family
		}
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family_context = utopia_manifesto_case_family }
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_clear_case_candidate_targets = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_case_candidate_targets
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_candidate_target
	}
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_case_candidate_targets
}

utopia_manifesto_prepare_case_candidate_targets = {
	utopia_manifesto_clear_case_candidate_targets = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			utopia_manifesto_has_any_case_deficit = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_need_case_active }
			NOT = { utopia_manifesto_has_active_external_case_term = yes }
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = { utopia_manifesto_case_candidate_is_valid = yes }
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_candidate_target
			ROOT = {
				add_to_array = {
					array = utopia_manifesto_case_candidate_targets
					value = PREV
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_clear_selected_country_target = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_selected_country_targets
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_selected_country_target
	}
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_selected_country_targets
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_selected_country_id
}

utopia_manifesto_save_from_as_selected_country_target = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			FROM = {
				utopia_manifesto_selected_country_target_is_valid = yes
				has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_candidate_target
			}
		}
		utopia_manifesto_clear_selected_country_target = yes
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_selected_country_id = FROM.id }
		add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_selected_country_targets value = FROM }
		FROM = { set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_selected_country_target }
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_clear_case_response_state = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_targets
		ROOT = {
			remove_targeted_decision = {
				target = PREV
				decision = mission_utopia_wait_for_need_answer
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_response_accept
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_response_counter
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_response_refuse
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_obsolete_case_treaty_offered
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_obsolete_case_treaty_accept
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_obsolete_case_treaty_refuse
	}
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_response_active
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_counteroffer_recorded
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_settlement_accepted
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_settlement_refused
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_ultimatum_refused
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_target_accepted
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_target_countered
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_target_refused
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_offer_purchase
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_offer_long_supply
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_offer_lease
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_offer_settlement_agreement
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_offer_joint_administration
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_offer_association
}

utopia_manifesto_activate_case_response_against_from = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			utopia_manifesto_active_need_case_is_valid = yes
			utopia_manifesto_from_is_selected_country_target = yes
			FROM = { NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } }
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_response_active
		activate_targeted_decision = { target = FROM decision = mission_utopia_wait_for_need_answer }
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_restore_case_offer_flag_from_method = {
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.purchase } } set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_offer_purchase }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.long_supply_contract } } set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_offer_long_supply }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.lease } } set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_offer_lease }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.settlement_agreement } } set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_offer_settlement_agreement }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.joint_administration } } set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_offer_joint_administration }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.association } } set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_offer_association }
}

utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_active_case_founder = {
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_active_case_target
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				is_in_array = {
					array = utopia_manifesto_case_founders
					value = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_founders value = ROOT }
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_unregister_root_as_active_case_founder = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_in_array = {
				array = utopia_manifesto_case_founders
				value = ROOT
			}
		}
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_founders value = ROOT }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = utopia_manifesto_case_founders^num
				value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_active_case_target
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_case_founders
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_clear_active_case_target_links = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_targets
		utopia_manifesto_unregister_root_as_active_case_founder = yes
	}
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_targets
}

utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_active_case_state_founder = {
	set_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_active_case_state
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				is_in_array = {
					array = utopia_manifesto_case_state_founders
					value = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_state_founders value = ROOT }
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_unregister_root_as_active_case_state_founder = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_in_array = {
				array = utopia_manifesto_case_state_founders
				value = ROOT
			}
		}
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_state_founders value = ROOT }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = utopia_manifesto_case_state_founders^num
				value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		clr_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_active_case_state
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_case_state_founders
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_clear_active_case_state_links = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_states
		utopia_manifesto_unregister_root_as_active_case_state_founder = yes
	}
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_states
}

utopia_manifesto_clear_active_need_case = {
	utopia_manifesto_clear_case_response_state = yes
	utopia_manifesto_remove_active_case_wargoal = yes
	utopia_manifesto_clear_active_case_created_diplomacy = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_active_mission = mission_utopia_need_case_expiry }
		remove_mission = mission_utopia_need_case_expiry
	}
	utopia_manifesto_clear_active_case_target_links = yes
	utopia_manifesto_clear_active_case_state_links = yes
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_ultimatum_issued
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_enforced
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_enforcement_war_active
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_wargoal_granted
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_need_case_active
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_domestic_review_proven
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_domestic_solution_found
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_domestic_solution_partial
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_domestic_solution_unavailable
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_domestic_solution_suppressed
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_target_relevance_proven
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_capital_proportionality_warning
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_trade_attempted
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_lease_attempted
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_settlement_attempted
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_joint_attempted
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_association_attempted
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_obsolete_case_review_pending
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_obsolete_case_treaty_pending
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_obsolete_case_review_due_to_deficit
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_obsolete_case_review_due_to_expiry
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_case_target_id
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_case_state_id
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_case_family
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_case_type
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_case_type_context
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_case_stage
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_case_method
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_case_integrity
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_case_local_support
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_case_offers
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_case_peaceful_attempt_count
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_case_expiry_days
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_case_unrelated_land
}

utopia_manifesto_prepare_case_integrity = {
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_integrity = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.default_integrity }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_local_support = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.default_local_support }
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_limit_law }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_integrity = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.mature_integrity_adjustment }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_expansion_law }
		subtract_from_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_integrity = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.mature_integrity_adjustment }
	}
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_offers = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_peaceful_attempt_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_unrelated_land = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
}

utopia_manifesto_consume_domestic_review_into_active_case = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			utopia_manifesto_domestic_review_authorizes_prepared_case = yes
			check_variable = {
				var = utopia_manifesto_domestic_review_family
				value = utopia_manifesto_case_family
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_domestic_review_proven
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_domestic_alternative_found }
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_domestic_solution_found
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_domestic_alternative_partial }
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_domestic_solution_partial
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_no_domestic_alternative_found }
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_domestic_solution_unavailable
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_domestic_review_solution_suppressed }
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_domestic_solution_suppressed
			subtract_from_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_integrity = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.suppressed_solution_integrity_loss }
		}
		else = {
			add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_integrity = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.domestic_review_integrity_gain }
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = utopia_manifesto_case_integrity
			min = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.integrity_minimum
			max = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.integrity_maximum
		}
		utopia_manifesto_clear_domestic_review_state = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_open_need_case_against_from = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			utopia_manifesto_domestic_review_authorizes_prepared_case = yes
			FROM = {
				utopia_manifesto_case_candidate_is_valid = yes
				has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_candidate_target
			}
		}
		utopia_manifesto_clear_active_need_case = yes
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_case_family_input } }
			set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family_input = utopia_manifesto_prepared_case_family }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_case_expiry_days_input } }
			set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_expiry_days_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.default_expiry_days }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_limit_law }
			set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_expiry_days_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.mature_limited_expiry_days }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_expansion_law }
			set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_expiry_days_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.mature_expansion_expiry_days }
		}
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_target_disappeared
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_target_succeeded_by_annexer
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_transfer_cancelled_to_preserve_target
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_expired
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_need_case_active
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_target_id = FROM.id }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family = utopia_manifesto_case_family_input }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family_context = utopia_manifesto_case_family_input }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_stage = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_stage.claim }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.unset }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_expiry_days = utopia_manifesto_case_expiry_days_input }
		add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_targets value = FROM }
		FROM = { utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_active_case_founder = yes }
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_case_integrity = yes
		utopia_manifesto_consume_domestic_review_into_active_case = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_case_candidate_targets = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_select_case_type_from_from_state = {
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_case_type
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_case_type_context
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_leased_island_case_required
			check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.maritime_and_settlement }
			FROM = { utopia_manifesto_state_supports_island_project_lease_case = yes }
		}
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_type = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_type.island_project_lease }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.provisioning }
			FROM = { utopia_manifesto_state_supports_essential_resource_case = yes }
		}
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_type = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_type.essential_resource }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.provisioning }
			FROM = { utopia_manifesto_state_supports_settlement_and_housing_case = yes }
		}
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_type = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_type.settlement_and_housing }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.provisioning }
			FROM = { utopia_manifesto_state_supports_reconstruction_zone_case = yes }
		}
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_type = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_type.reconstruction_zone }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.workshops }
			FROM = { utopia_manifesto_state_supports_essential_resource_case = yes }
		}
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_type = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_type.essential_resource }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.workshops }
			FROM = { utopia_manifesto_state_supports_reconstruction_zone_case = yes }
		}
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_type = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_type.reconstruction_zone }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.civic_works }
			FROM = { utopia_manifesto_state_supports_defensive_corridor_case = yes }
		}
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_type = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_type.defensive_corridor }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.civic_works }
			FROM = { utopia_manifesto_state_supports_reconstruction_zone_case = yes }
		}
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_type = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_type.reconstruction_zone }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.learning_and_care }
			FROM = { utopia_manifesto_state_supports_settlement_and_housing_case = yes }
		}
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_type = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_type.settlement_and_housing }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.learning_and_care }
			FROM = { utopia_manifesto_state_supports_reconstruction_zone_case = yes }
		}
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_type = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_type.reconstruction_zone }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.maritime_and_settlement }
			FROM = { utopia_manifesto_state_supports_island_or_capital_refuge_case = yes }
		}
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_type = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_type.island_or_capital_refuge }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.maritime_and_settlement }
			FROM = { utopia_manifesto_state_supports_port_access_case = yes }
		}
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_type = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_type.port_access }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.maritime_and_settlement }
			FROM = { utopia_manifesto_state_supports_settlement_and_housing_case = yes }
		}
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_type = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_type.settlement_and_housing }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.defense_and_watches }
			FROM = { utopia_manifesto_state_supports_defensive_corridor_case = yes }
		}
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_type = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_type.defensive_corridor }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.defense_and_watches }
			FROM = { utopia_manifesto_state_supports_island_or_capital_refuge_case = yes }
		}
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_type = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_type.island_or_capital_refuge }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_case_type }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_type_context = utopia_manifesto_case_type }
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_set_from_state_as_case_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_from_state_is_valid_case_state = yes }
		utopia_manifesto_select_case_type_from_from_state = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = utopia_manifesto_case_type
				FROM = { utopia_manifesto_case_state_is_relevant = yes }
			}
			utopia_manifesto_clear_active_case_state_links = yes
			set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_state_id = FROM.id }
			add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_states value = FROM }
			FROM = { utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_active_case_state_founder = yes }
			set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_stage = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_stage.assessment }
		}
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_assess_selected_case_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_assessed_need_case_is_valid = yes }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_target_relevance_proven
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_integrity = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.state_relevance_integrity_gain }
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_of_scopes = {
					array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_states
					is_capital = yes
				}
				NOT = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_type = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_type.island_or_capital_refuge } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_type = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_type.reconstruction_zone } }
			}
			subtract_from_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_integrity = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.capital_state_integrity_loss }
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_capital_proportionality_warning
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = utopia_manifesto_case_integrity
			min = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.integrity_minimum
			max = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.integrity_maximum
		}
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_case_local_support_delta = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_case_local_support_delta } }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_local_support_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	}
	add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_local_support = utopia_manifesto_case_local_support_delta }
	clamp_variable = {
		var = utopia_manifesto_case_local_support
		min = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.local_support_minimum
		max = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.local_support_maximum
	}
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_local_support_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
}

utopia_manifesto_record_prepared_case_method = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_case_method_input }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = utopia_manifesto_case_method_input }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.unset }
}

utopia_manifesto_record_case_ladder_skip = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_ladder_restoration_in_progress }
			OR = {
				AND = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.lease }
						check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.settlement_agreement }
					}
					NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_trade_attempted }
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.joint_administration }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_lease_attempted }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_settlement_attempted }
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.association }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_joint_attempted }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_settlement_attempted }
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.ultimatum }
					check_variable = {
						var = utopia_manifesto_case_peaceful_attempt_count
						value = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.minimum_peaceful_attempts_before_ultimatum
						compare = less_than
					}
				}
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_conduct_skipped_peaceful_case_step
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_foreign_opposition_from_case_shortcut
		subtract_from_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_integrity = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.peaceful_ladder_skip_integrity_loss }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_step_loss }
		utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_ledger_delta = yes
		clamp_variable = {
			var = utopia_manifesto_case_integrity
			min = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.integrity_minimum
			max = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.integrity_maximum
		}
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_record_case_ladder_attempt = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.purchase }
				check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.long_supply_contract }
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_trade_attempted }
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_trade_attempted
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_peaceful_attempt_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.lease }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_lease_attempted }
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_lease_attempted
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_peaceful_attempt_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.settlement_agreement }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_settlement_attempted }
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_settlement_attempted
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_peaceful_attempt_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.joint_administration }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_joint_attempted }
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_joint_attempted
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_peaceful_attempt_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.association }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_association_attempted }
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_association_attempted
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_peaceful_attempt_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one }
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_record_case_offer = {
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_active_need_case_is_valid = yes }
		utopia_manifesto_record_case_ladder_skip = yes
		utopia_manifesto_record_case_ladder_attempt = yes
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_case_offer_input } } set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_offer_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.offer_required } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_offers = utopia_manifesto_case_offer_input }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_local_support = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.offer_support_gain }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_stage = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_stage.offer }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.unset } }
			set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.long_supply_contract }
		}
		clamp_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_case_local_support min = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.local_support_minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.local_support_maximum }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_offer_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.offer_required }
}

utopia_manifesto_relieve_active_case_deficit = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_case_deficit_relief_input } }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_deficit_relief_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.stewardship_deficit_relief }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.provisioning } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_policy_adjustment = utopia_manifesto_case_deficit_relief_input }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.workshops } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_policy_adjustment = utopia_manifesto_case_deficit_relief_input }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.civic_works } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_policy_adjustment = utopia_manifesto_case_deficit_relief_input }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.learning_and_care } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_policy_adjustment = utopia_manifesto_case_deficit_relief_input }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.maritime_and_settlement } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_policy_adjustment = utopia_manifesto_case_deficit_relief_input }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_family = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.defense_and_watches } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_policy_adjustment = utopia_manifesto_case_deficit_relief_input }
	}
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_calling_state = yes
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_deficit_relief_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
}

utopia_manifesto_apply_active_case_state_baseline = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_states
		set_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_obligation_state
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_state_stewardship_supply = constant:utopia_manifesto_state_modifier_value.stewardship_supply }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_state_stewardship_resistance = constant:utopia_manifesto_state_modifier_value.stewardship_resistance }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_obligation_state } } }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_obligation_state }
		}
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_apply_purchase_settlement = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_states
		set_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_purchase_settlement_state
		transfer_state_to = ROOT
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_apply_lease_settlement = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_states
		set_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_lease_administration_state
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_state_lease_supply = constant:utopia_manifesto_state_modifier_value.lease_supply }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_state_lease_resources = constant:utopia_manifesto_state_modifier_value.lease_resources }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_lease_administration_state }
		set_state_controller_to = ROOT
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_targets
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { gives_military_access_to = ROOT } }
			give_military_access = ROOT
			if = {
				limit = { gives_military_access_to = ROOT }
				set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_created_access_to_founder
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_created_access_founders value = ROOT } } }
					add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_created_access_founders value = ROOT }
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				utopia_manifesto_has_event15_access_creator_for_root = yes
				NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_created_access_founders value = ROOT } }
			}
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_created_access_to_founder
			add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_created_access_founders value = ROOT }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_guaranteed_by = ROOT } }
			ROOT = { give_guarantee = PREV }
			if = {
				limit = { is_guaranteed_by = ROOT }
				ROOT = { set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_created_guarantee_to_target }
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_created_guarantee_founders value = ROOT } } }
					add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_created_guarantee_founders value = ROOT }
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				utopia_manifesto_has_event15_founder_guarantee_creator_for_root = yes
				NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_created_guarantee_founders value = ROOT } }
			}
			add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_created_guarantee_founders value = ROOT }
			ROOT = { set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_created_guarantee_to_target }
		}
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_start_island_project_lease_from_active_case = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			utopia_manifesto_active_need_case_is_valid = yes
			check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_type = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_type.island_project_lease }
			check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.lease }
		}
		utopia_manifesto_clear_island_project_scopes = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_temporary_stewardship_state_modifiers = yes
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_targets
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lessor
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lessor_founders value = ROOT } } }
				add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lessor_founders value = ROOT }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_created_access_founders value = ROOT } }
				remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_created_access_founders value = ROOT }
				set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_project_created_access_to_founder
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_created_access_founders value = ROOT } } }
					add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_created_access_founders value = ROOT }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_case_created_access_founders^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals } }
					clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_created_access_to_founder
					clear_array = utopia_manifesto_case_created_access_founders
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_created_guarantee_founders value = ROOT } }
				remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_created_guarantee_founders value = ROOT }
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_created_guarantee_founders value = ROOT } } }
					add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_created_guarantee_founders value = ROOT }
				}
				ROOT = {
					clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_created_guarantee_to_target
					set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_project_created_guarantee_to_lessor
				}
			}
			ROOT = {
				add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lessor_targets value = PREV }
				set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_island_project_lessor_id = PREV.id }
			}
		}
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_states
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lease_state_founders value = ROOT } } }
				add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lease_state_founders value = ROOT }
			}
			set_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_project_site
			set_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_project_primary_site
			set_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_leased_island_project_site
			set_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_site_secured
			set_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lease_state
			set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_island_project_lease_supply = constant:utopia_manifesto_state_modifier_value.lease_supply }
			set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_island_project_lease_resources = constant:utopia_manifesto_state_modifier_value.lease_resources }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lease_state } } }
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lease_state }
			}
			ROOT = {
				add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_states value = PREV }
				add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_primary_states value = PREV }
				set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_island_project_primary_state_id = PREV.id }
			}
		}
		utopia_manifesto_record_external_case_completion = yes
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lease_active
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_site_secured
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_project_stranded
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_expired
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_return_deferred
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_attempted
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_leased_island_case_required
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_island_lease_days = constant:utopia_manifesto_island_project_limits.lease_initial_days }
		activate_mission = mission_utopia_hold_island_project_lease
		utopia_manifesto_clear_active_need_case = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_selected_country_target = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_dominant_case_family = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_case_candidate_targets = yes
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_focus_visibility = yes
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_formation_proof = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_extend_island_project_lease = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lease_active
			has_active_mission = mission_utopia_hold_island_project_lease
			check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_island_lease_extension_input > constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
		}
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_island_lease_days = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_extension_input }
		meta_effect = {
			text = { add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = mission_utopia_hold_island_project_lease days = [DAYS] } }
			DAYS = "[?utopia_manifesto_island_lease_extension_input|.0]"
		}
	}
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_island_lease_extension_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
}

# Lessor scope; ROOT is the exact founder whose renewal response is consumed
# or cancelled. Pair arrays prevent one lessor's simultaneous answer from
# being observed by another Event 15 founder.
utopia_manifesto_clear_root_island_lease_response = {
	remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_accept_founders value = ROOT }
	remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_counter_founders value = ROOT }
	remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_refuse_founders value = ROOT }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_accept_founders^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals } }
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_accept
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_accept_founders
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_counter_founders^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals } }
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_counter
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_counter_founders
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_refuse_founders^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals } }
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_refuse
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_refuse_founders
	}
}

# Lessor scope; ROOT is the exact founder. Answer cleanup is deliberately
# separate from reservation cleanup so cancellation can invalidate an open
# popup without freeing its same-pair slot.
utopia_manifesto_clear_root_island_lease_renewal_reservation = {
	remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_pending_founders value = ROOT }
	remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_invalidated_founders value = ROOT }
	ROOT = {
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_pending_targets value = PREV }
	}
}

# Lessor scope; ROOT is the exact founder whose live lease is being cancelled
# or replanned. The answer becomes unusable, but both reservation directions
# remain until the old .213 popup resolves through .214.
utopia_manifesto_invalidate_root_island_lease_renewal_request = {
	utopia_manifesto_clear_root_island_lease_response = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_pending_founders value = ROOT }
			ROOT = {
				is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_pending_targets value = PREV }
			}
			NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_invalidated_founders value = ROOT } }
		}
		add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_invalidated_founders value = ROOT }
	}
}

# Current country can hold founder-side targets, lessor-side founders, or both.
# Terminal teardown snapshots both roles before removing reverse links so no
# other country retains an outstanding slot pointing at the terminal actor.
utopia_manifesto_clear_all_island_lease_renewal_reservations = {
	clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_lessor_snapshot
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_pending_targets
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_lessor_snapshot value = THIS } } }
			add_to_temp_array = { utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_lessor_snapshot = THIS }
		}
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_lessor_snapshot
		utopia_manifesto_clear_root_island_lease_response = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_root_island_lease_renewal_reservation = yes
	}
	clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_lessor_snapshot

	clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_founder_snapshot
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_pending_founders
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_founder_snapshot value = THIS } } }
			add_to_temp_array = { utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_founder_snapshot = THIS }
		}
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_invalidated_founders
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_founder_snapshot value = THIS } } }
			add_to_temp_array = { utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_founder_snapshot = THIS }
		}
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_accept_founders
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_founder_snapshot value = THIS } } }
			add_to_temp_array = { utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_founder_snapshot = THIS }
		}
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_counter_founders
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_founder_snapshot value = THIS } } }
			add_to_temp_array = { utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_founder_snapshot = THIS }
		}
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_refuse_founders
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_founder_snapshot value = THIS } } }
			add_to_temp_array = { utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_founder_snapshot = THIS }
		}
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_founder_snapshot
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_pending_targets value = ROOT }
		ROOT = {
			remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_pending_founders value = PREV }
			remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_invalidated_founders value = PREV }
			remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_accept_founders value = PREV }
			remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_counter_founders value = PREV }
			remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_refuse_founders value = PREV }
		}
	}
	clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_founder_snapshot
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_pending_targets
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_pending_founders
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_invalidated_founders
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_accept_founders
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_counter_founders
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_refuse_founders
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_accept
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_counter
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_renewal_refuse
}

utopia_manifesto_return_island_project_lease = {
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_return_in_progress
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lessor_targets
		utopia_manifesto_invalidate_root_island_lease_renewal_request = yes
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lessor_targets
		save_event_target_as = utopia_manifesto_island_project_return_lessor
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = utopia_manifesto_island_project_return_lessor }
		event_target:utopia_manifesto_island_project_return_lessor = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_created_access_founders value = ROOT } }
				remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_created_access_founders value = ROOT }
				if = {
					limit = {
						gives_military_access_to = ROOT
						NOT = { utopia_manifesto_has_event15_access_creator_for_root = yes }
					}
					diplomatic_relation = { country = ROOT relation = military_access active = no }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_created_guarantee_founders value = ROOT } }
				remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_created_guarantee_founders value = ROOT }
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_guaranteed_by = ROOT
						NOT = { utopia_manifesto_has_event15_founder_guarantee_creator_for_root = yes }
					}
					ROOT = { diplomatic_relation = { country = PREV relation = guarantee active = no } }
				}
			}
			remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lessor_founders value = ROOT }
			remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_compact_founders value = ROOT }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_island_project_created_access_founders^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals } }
				clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_project_created_access_to_founder
				clear_array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_created_access_founders
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lessor_founders^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals } }
				clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lessor
				clear_array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lessor_founders
			}
			utopia_manifesto_remove_root_compact_recognition_if_unbased = yes
		}
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_states
			remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lease_state_founders value = ROOT }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lease_state_founders^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals } }
				clr_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lease_state
				if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lease_state } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lease_state } }
				clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lease_supply
				clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lease_resources
				clear_array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lease_state_founders
				if = {
					limit = {
						owner = {
							is_in_array = {
								array = ROOT.utopia_manifesto_island_project_lessor_targets
								value = THIS
							}
						}
						is_controlled_by = ROOT
					}
					set_state_controller_to = event_target:utopia_manifesto_island_project_return_lessor
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						owner = {
							is_in_array = {
								array = ROOT.utopia_manifesto_island_project_lessor_targets
								value = THIS
							}
						}
					}
					ROOT = { set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_return_deferred }
				}
				else = {
					ROOT = { set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_ownership_reconciliation_required }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_ownership_reconciliation_required
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_return_deferred
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_states
			remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lease_state_founders value = ROOT }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lease_state_founders^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals } }
				clr_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lease_state
				if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lease_state } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lease_state } }
				clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lease_supply
				clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lease_resources
				clear_array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lease_state_founders
			}
		}
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lessor_targets
		if = {
			limit = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_created_access_founders value = ROOT } }
			remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_created_access_founders value = ROOT }
			if = {
				limit = {
					gives_military_access_to = ROOT
					NOT = { utopia_manifesto_has_event15_access_creator_for_root = yes }
				}
				diplomatic_relation = { country = ROOT relation = military_access active = no }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_created_guarantee_founders value = ROOT } }
			remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_created_guarantee_founders value = ROOT }
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_guaranteed_by = ROOT
					NOT = { utopia_manifesto_has_event15_founder_guarantee_creator_for_root = yes }
				}
				ROOT = { diplomatic_relation = { country = PREV relation = guarantee active = no } }
			}
		}
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lessor_founders value = ROOT }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lessor_founders^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals } }
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lessor
			clear_array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lessor_founders
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_island_project_created_access_founders^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals } }
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_project_created_access_to_founder
			clear_array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_created_access_founders
		}
	}
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lessor_targets
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lessor_id
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_project_created_guarantee_to_lessor
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lease_active
	if = { limit = { has_active_mission = mission_utopia_hold_island_project_lease } remove_mission = mission_utopia_hold_island_project_lease }
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_return_in_progress
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_league_state = yes
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_formation_proof = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_expire_island_project_lease = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lease_active }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_lease_expired
		utopia_manifesto_return_island_project_lease = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_island_project_scopes = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_island_project_downstream_proof = yes
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_project_stranded
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_leased_island_case_required
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_dominant_case_family = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_case_candidate_targets = yes
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_step }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_plenty_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_step_loss }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_step_loss }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_assignment_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_nudge }
		utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_ledger_delta = yes
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_focus_visibility = yes
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_formation_proof = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_apply_joint_administration_settlement = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_states
		set_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_joint_administration_state
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_state_joint_supply = constant:utopia_manifesto_state_modifier_value.joint_supply }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_state_joint_construction = constant:utopia_manifesto_state_modifier_value.joint_construction }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_joint_administration_state }
		set_state_controller_to = ROOT
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_targets
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { gives_military_access_to = ROOT } }
			give_military_access = ROOT
			if = {
				limit = { gives_military_access_to = ROOT }
				set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_created_access_to_founder
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_created_access_founders value = ROOT } } }
					add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_created_access_founders value = ROOT }
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				utopia_manifesto_has_event15_access_creator_for_root = yes
				NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_created_access_founders value = ROOT } }
			}
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_created_access_to_founder
			add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_created_access_founders value = ROOT }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_guaranteed_by = ROOT } }
			ROOT = { give_guarantee = PREV }
			if = {
				limit = { is_guaranteed_by = ROOT }
				ROOT = { set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_created_guarantee_to_target }
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_created_guarantee_founders value = ROOT } } }
					add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_created_guarantee_founders value = ROOT }
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				utopia_manifesto_has_event15_founder_guarantee_creator_for_root = yes
				NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_created_guarantee_founders value = ROOT } }
			}
			add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_created_guarantee_founders value = ROOT }
			ROOT = { set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_created_guarantee_to_target }
		}
		ROOT = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { gives_military_access_to = PREV } }
				give_military_access = PREV
				if = {
					limit = { gives_military_access_to = PREV }
					set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_created_reciprocal_access
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_apply_long_supply_contract = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_states
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_resources_rights = { receiver = ROOT } } }
			owner = {
				give_resource_rights = {
					receiver = ROOT
					state = PREV
				}
			}
			add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_created_resource_right_founders value = ROOT }
		}
	}
}

# Target scope; ROOT is the exact Event 15 founder. A live Event 15 creator may
# share ownership of an existing Event 15 relation, while an un-attributed
# pre-existing relation is never claimed.
utopia_manifesto_apply_root_association_diplomacy = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { gives_military_access_to = ROOT } }
		give_military_access = ROOT
		if = {
			limit = { gives_military_access_to = ROOT }
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_access_created
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_association_created_access_founders value = ROOT } } }
				add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_association_created_access_founders value = ROOT }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			utopia_manifesto_has_event15_access_creator_for_root = yes
			NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_association_created_access_founders value = ROOT } }
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_access_created
		add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_association_created_access_founders value = ROOT }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { is_guaranteed_by = ROOT } }
		ROOT = { give_guarantee = PREV }
		if = {
			limit = { is_guaranteed_by = ROOT }
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_guarantee_created
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_association_created_guarantee_founders value = ROOT } } }
				add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_association_created_guarantee_founders value = ROOT }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			utopia_manifesto_has_event15_founder_guarantee_creator_for_root = yes
			NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_association_created_guarantee_founders value = ROOT } }
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_guarantee_created
		add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_association_created_guarantee_founders value = ROOT }
	}
}

# Scope-neutral exact-pair cleanup. The current association source is removed
# before testing the remaining creator arrays, so another live Event 15 package
# keeps the shared relation intact.
utopia_manifesto_remove_saved_pair_association_diplomacy = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = utopia_manifesto_diplomacy_founder_scope
			has_event_target = utopia_manifesto_diplomacy_partner_scope
		}
		event_target:utopia_manifesto_diplomacy_partner_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_association_created_access_founders value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_diplomacy_founder_scope } }
				remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_association_created_access_founders value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_diplomacy_founder_scope }
				if = {
					limit = {
						gives_military_access_to = event_target:utopia_manifesto_diplomacy_founder_scope
						NOT = { utopia_manifesto_saved_diplomacy_pair_has_access_creator = yes }
					}
					diplomatic_relation = { country = event_target:utopia_manifesto_diplomacy_founder_scope relation = military_access active = no }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_association_created_guarantee_founders value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_diplomacy_founder_scope } }
				remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_association_created_guarantee_founders value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_diplomacy_founder_scope }
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_guaranteed_by = event_target:utopia_manifesto_diplomacy_founder_scope
						NOT = { utopia_manifesto_saved_diplomacy_pair_has_founder_guarantee_creator = yes }
					}
					event_target:utopia_manifesto_diplomacy_founder_scope = {
						diplomatic_relation = { country = event_target:utopia_manifesto_diplomacy_partner_scope relation = guarantee active = no }
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_association_created_access_founders^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals } }
				clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_access_created
				clear_array = utopia_manifesto_association_created_access_founders
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_association_created_guarantee_founders^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals } }
				clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_guarantee_created
				clear_array = utopia_manifesto_association_created_guarantee_founders
			}
		}
	}
}

# Target scope; ROOT is the exact Event 15 founder whose association is ending.
utopia_manifesto_remove_root_association_diplomacy = {
	save_event_target_as = utopia_manifesto_diplomacy_partner_scope
	ROOT = { save_event_target_as = utopia_manifesto_diplomacy_founder_scope }
	utopia_manifesto_remove_saved_pair_association_diplomacy = yes
}

# Target scope; ROOT is the founder that accepted an independence-with-pact
# vote. Unlike association this creates no access and no local charter. The
# guarantee is attributed per founder so terminal cleanup never removes a
# pre-existing or another founder's relation.
utopia_manifesto_apply_root_stewardship_autonomy_pact = {
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_autonomy_partner
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				is_in_array = {
					array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_autonomy_founders
					value = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_autonomy_founders value = ROOT }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { is_guaranteed_by = ROOT } }
		ROOT = { give_guarantee = PREV }
		if = {
			limit = { is_guaranteed_by = ROOT }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_autonomy_created_guarantee_founders value = ROOT } } }
				add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_autonomy_created_guarantee_founders value = ROOT }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			utopia_manifesto_has_event15_founder_guarantee_creator_for_root = yes
			NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_autonomy_created_guarantee_founders value = ROOT } }
		}
		add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_autonomy_created_guarantee_founders value = ROOT }
	}
	add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = utopia_manifesto_opinion_partnership }
	ROOT = {
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = PREV modifier = utopia_manifesto_opinion_partnership }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_autonomy_partners value = PREV } } }
			add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_autonomy_partners value = PREV }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_compacts value = PREV } } }
			add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_compacts value = PREV }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_external_partners value = PREV } } }
			add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_external_partners value = PREV }
		}
	}
}

# Target scope; ROOT is the founder whose autonomy pact is ending.
utopia_manifesto_remove_root_stewardship_autonomy_pact = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_in_array = {
				array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_autonomy_created_guarantee_founders
				value = ROOT
			}
		}
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_autonomy_created_guarantee_founders value = ROOT }
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_guaranteed_by = ROOT
				NOT = { utopia_manifesto_has_event15_founder_guarantee_creator_for_root = yes }
			}
			ROOT = { diplomatic_relation = { country = PREV relation = guarantee active = no } }
		}
	}
	remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_autonomy_founders value = ROOT }
	ROOT = { remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_autonomy_partners value = PREV } }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_autonomy_founders^num
				value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_autonomy_partner
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_autonomy_founders
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_autonomy_created_guarantee_founders^num
				value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_autonomy_created_guarantee_founders
	}
	utopia_manifesto_remove_root_compact_recognition_if_unbased = yes
}

# Partner scope; ROOT is the exact founder. Recognition persists only while
# that pair still has a recorded compact source or another live external-network
# basis. Removing one source never erases another founder's or route's pact.
utopia_manifesto_remove_root_compact_recognition_if_unbased = {
	if = {
		limit = {
		NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_compact_founders value = ROOT } }
		NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_compact_founders value = ROOT } }
		NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_settlement_compact_founders value = ROOT } }
		NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_compact_founders value = ROOT } }
		NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_autonomy_founders value = ROOT } }
		}
		ROOT = { remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_compacts value = PREV } }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { utopia_manifesto_has_other_external_recognition_basis_for_root = yes } }
			ROOT = { remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_external_partners value = PREV } }
		}
	}
}

# Uses the exact event targets saved as utopia_manifesto_recognition_founder_scope
# and utopia_manifesto_recognition_partner_scope. Unlike source-specific cleanup,
# this removes only the visible recognition after every independent pair basis is
# gone, so ending an association cannot erase a surviving compact or League role.
utopia_manifesto_remove_saved_pair_recognition_if_unbased = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = utopia_manifesto_recognition_founder_scope
			has_event_target = utopia_manifesto_recognition_partner_scope
			NOT = { utopia_manifesto_saved_recognition_pair_has_external_basis = yes }
		}
		event_target:utopia_manifesto_recognition_founder_scope = {
			remove_from_array = {
				array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_external_partners
				value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_recognition_partner_scope
			}
		}
	}
}

# Partner scope; ROOT is the exact founder. Revolt mediation by association is
# a durable recognition source distinct from an ordinary charter, compact, or
# League role, so both sides retain an exact reverse index.
utopia_manifesto_register_root_colony_association = {
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_colony_association_partner
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_colony_association_founders value = ROOT } } }
		add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_colony_association_founders value = ROOT }
	}
	ROOT = {
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_colony_association_partners value = PREV } } }
			add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_colony_association_partners value = PREV }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_external_partners value = PREV } } }
			add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_external_partners value = PREV }
		}
	}
	utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_league_founder = yes
}

# Partner scope; ROOT is the exact founder whose mediated colony association is
# ending through annexation or founder teardown. Other recognition bases remain.
utopia_manifesto_remove_root_colony_association = {
	remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_colony_association_founders value = ROOT }
	ROOT = { remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_colony_association_partners value = PREV } }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = utopia_manifesto_colony_association_founders^num
				value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_colony_association_partner
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_colony_association_founders
	}
	utopia_manifesto_remove_root_compact_recognition_if_unbased = yes
	utopia_manifesto_unregister_root_as_league_founder_if_unrelated = yes
}

# Partner scope; ROOT is the founder whose non-autonomy compact sources are
# ending through disable, annexation, or an expiring lease.
utopia_manifesto_remove_root_non_autonomy_compact_sources = {
	remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_compact_founders value = ROOT }
	remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_compact_founders value = ROOT }
	remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_settlement_compact_founders value = ROOT }
	remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_compact_founders value = ROOT }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_case_compact_founders^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals } }
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_case_compact_founders
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_island_project_compact_founders^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals } }
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_compact_founders
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_settlement_compact_founders^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals } }
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_settlement_compact_partner
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_settlement_compact_founders
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_compact_founders^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals } }
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_compact_partner
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_compact_founders
	}
	utopia_manifesto_remove_root_compact_recognition_if_unbased = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_apply_association_settlement = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_targets
		utopia_manifesto_apply_root_association_diplomacy = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_register_root_settlement_agreement_target = {
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_target
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_founders value = ROOT } } }
		add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_founders value = ROOT }
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_unregister_root_settlement_agreement_target = {
	remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_founders value = ROOT }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_founders^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals } }
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_target
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_founders
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_register_root_settlement_agreement_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_state_founders value = ROOT } } }
		add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_state_founders value = ROOT }
	}
	set_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_state
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_state_settlement_supply = constant:utopia_manifesto_state_modifier_value.settlement_supply }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_state_settlement_construction = constant:utopia_manifesto_state_modifier_value.settlement_construction }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_state } } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_state }
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_unregister_root_settlement_agreement_state = {
	remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_state_founders value = ROOT }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_state_founders^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals } }
		clr_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_state
		if = {
			limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_state } }
