			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_state }
		}
		clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_state_settlement_supply
		clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_state_settlement_construction
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_state_founders
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_clear_settlement_agreement_runtime = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_targets
		utopia_manifesto_unregister_root_settlement_agreement_target = yes
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_states
		utopia_manifesto_unregister_root_settlement_agreement_state = yes
	}
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_targets
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_states
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_active
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_target_id
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_state_id
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_family
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_type
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_days
}

utopia_manifesto_cleanup_settlement_agreement = {
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_cleanup_in_progress
	if = {
		limit = { has_active_mission = mission_utopia_fulfil_settlement_agreement }
		remove_mission = mission_utopia_fulfil_settlement_agreement
	}
	utopia_manifesto_clear_settlement_agreement_runtime = yes
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_cleanup_in_progress
}

utopia_manifesto_relieve_stored_case_family = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_stored_family_input } } set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_stored_family_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.none } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_stored_relief_input } } set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_stored_relief_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero } }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_stored_family_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.provisioning } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_provisioning_policy_adjustment = utopia_manifesto_stored_relief_input }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_stored_family_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.workshops } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_workshops_policy_adjustment = utopia_manifesto_stored_relief_input }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_stored_family_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.civic_works } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_civic_works_policy_adjustment = utopia_manifesto_stored_relief_input }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_stored_family_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.learning_and_care } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_learning_and_care_policy_adjustment = utopia_manifesto_stored_relief_input }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_stored_family_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.maritime_and_settlement } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_maritime_and_settlement_policy_adjustment = utopia_manifesto_stored_relief_input }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_stored_family_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.defense_and_watches } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_calling_defense_and_watches_policy_adjustment = utopia_manifesto_stored_relief_input }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_stored_family_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_calling_family.none }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_stored_relief_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_calling_state = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_relieve_settlement_agreement_deficit = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_relief_input } } set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_relief_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero } }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_stored_family_input = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_family }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_stored_relief_input = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_relief_input }
	utopia_manifesto_relieve_stored_case_family = yes
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_relief_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
}

utopia_manifesto_relieve_long_supply_contract_deficit = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_relief_input } } set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_relief_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero } }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_stored_family_input = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_family }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_stored_relief_input = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_relief_input }
	utopia_manifesto_relieve_stored_case_family = yes
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_relief_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
}

utopia_manifesto_relieve_association_duties_deficit = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_relief_input } } set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_association_duties_relief_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero } }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_stored_family_input = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_family }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_stored_relief_input = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_relief_input }
	utopia_manifesto_relieve_stored_case_family = yes
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_association_duties_relief_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
}

utopia_manifesto_start_settlement_agreement_from_active_case = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			utopia_manifesto_active_need_case_is_valid = yes
			check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.settlement_agreement }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_active }
		}
		utopia_manifesto_clear_settlement_agreement_runtime = yes
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_active
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_target_id = utopia_manifesto_case_target_id }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_state_id = utopia_manifesto_case_state_id }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_family = utopia_manifesto_case_family }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_type = utopia_manifesto_case_type }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_targets
			utopia_manifesto_register_root_settlement_agreement_target = yes
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = utopia_manifesto_opinion_partnership }
			ROOT = { add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_targets value = PREV } }
		}
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_states
			utopia_manifesto_register_root_settlement_agreement_state = yes
			ROOT = { add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_states value = PREV } }
		}
		utopia_manifesto_reconcile_league_reverse_links = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_settlement_agreement_duration = yes
		activate_mission = mission_utopia_fulfil_settlement_agreement
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_relief_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.settlement_agreement_initial_relief }
		utopia_manifesto_relieve_settlement_agreement_deficit = yes
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_nudge_loss }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_plenty_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_nudge }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_step }
		utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_ledger_delta = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_active_need_case = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_selected_country_target = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_dominant_case_family = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_case_candidate_targets = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_complete_settlement_agreement = {
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_is_valid = yes }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_targets
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_settlement_compact_partner
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_settlement_compact_founders value = ROOT } } }
				add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_settlement_compact_founders value = ROOT }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = ROOT.utopia_manifesto_recognized_compacts value = THIS } } }
				ROOT = { add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_compacts value = PREV } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = ROOT.utopia_manifesto_recognized_external_partners value = THIS } } }
				ROOT = { add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_external_partners value = PREV } }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_fulfilled
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_first_external_case_complete
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_first_external_case_resolved
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_consensual_partner_proven
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_first_external_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.settlement_agreement }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_external_case_count } } set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_external_case_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_external_case_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_relief_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.settlement_agreement_completion_relief }
		utopia_manifesto_relieve_settlement_agreement_deficit = yes
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_achievement_case_target_input = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_target_id }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_achievement_case_method_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.settlement_agreement }
		utopia_manifesto_record_achievement_peaceful_case_resolution = yes
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_step_loss }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_plenty_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_step }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_shift }
		utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_ledger_delta = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_settlement_agreement_runtime = yes
		utopia_manifesto_reconcile_league_reverse_links = yes
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_league_state = yes
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_formation_proof = yes
	}
	else = { utopia_manifesto_fail_settlement_agreement = yes }
}

utopia_manifesto_fail_settlement_agreement = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_active }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_failed
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_relief_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.settlement_agreement_failure_reversal }
		utopia_manifesto_relieve_settlement_agreement_deficit = yes
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_step }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_plenty_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_nudge_loss }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_shift_loss }
		utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_ledger_delta = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_settlement_agreement_runtime = yes
		utopia_manifesto_reconcile_league_reverse_links = yes
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_league_state = yes
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_formation_proof = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_register_root_long_supply_contract_target = {
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_target
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_founders value = ROOT } } }
		add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_founders value = ROOT }
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_unregister_root_long_supply_contract_target = {
	remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_founders value = ROOT }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_founders^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals } }
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_target
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_founders
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_register_root_long_supply_contract_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_state_founders value = ROOT } } }
		add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_state_founders value = ROOT }
	}
	set_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_state
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_state_long_supply_resources = constant:utopia_manifesto_state_modifier_value.long_supply_resources }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_state } } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_state }
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_unregister_root_long_supply_contract_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_created_resource_right_founders value = ROOT } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_resources_rights = { receiver = ROOT } }
			ROOT = { remove_resource_rights = PREV }
		}
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_created_resource_right_founders value = ROOT }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_created_resource_right_founders^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals } }
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_created_resource_right_founders
	}
	remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_state_founders value = ROOT }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_state_founders^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals } }
		clr_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_state
		if = {
			limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_state } }
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_state }
		}
		clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_state_long_supply_resources
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_state_founders
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_clear_long_supply_contract_runtime = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_targets
		utopia_manifesto_unregister_root_long_supply_contract_target = yes
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_states
		utopia_manifesto_unregister_root_long_supply_contract_state = yes
	}
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_targets
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_states
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_active
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_target_id
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_state_id
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_family
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_type
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_days
}

utopia_manifesto_cleanup_long_supply_contract = {
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_cleanup_in_progress
	if = {
		limit = { has_active_mission = mission_utopia_fulfil_long_supply_contract }
		remove_mission = mission_utopia_fulfil_long_supply_contract
	}
	utopia_manifesto_clear_long_supply_contract_runtime = yes
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_cleanup_in_progress
}

utopia_manifesto_start_long_supply_contract_from_active_case = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			utopia_manifesto_active_need_case_is_valid = yes
			check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.long_supply_contract }
			NOT = { utopia_manifesto_has_active_external_case_term = yes }
		}
		utopia_manifesto_clear_long_supply_contract_runtime = yes
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_active
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_target_id = utopia_manifesto_case_target_id }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_state_id = utopia_manifesto_case_state_id }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_family = utopia_manifesto_case_family }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_type = utopia_manifesto_case_type }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_targets
			utopia_manifesto_register_root_long_supply_contract_target = yes
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = utopia_manifesto_opinion_partnership }
			ROOT = { add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_targets value = PREV } }
		}
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_states
			utopia_manifesto_register_root_long_supply_contract_state = yes
			ROOT = { add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_states value = PREV } }
		}
		utopia_manifesto_apply_long_supply_contract = yes
		utopia_manifesto_reconcile_league_reverse_links = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_long_supply_contract_duration = yes
		activate_mission = mission_utopia_fulfil_long_supply_contract
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_relief_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.long_supply_initial_relief }
		utopia_manifesto_relieve_long_supply_contract_deficit = yes
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_nudge_loss }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_plenty_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_step }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_nudge }
		utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_ledger_delta = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_active_need_case = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_selected_country_target = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_case_candidate_targets = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_complete_long_supply_contract = {
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_is_valid = yes }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_targets
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_compact_partner
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_compact_founders value = ROOT } } }
				add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_compact_founders value = ROOT }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = ROOT.utopia_manifesto_recognized_compacts value = THIS } } }
				ROOT = { add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_compacts value = PREV } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = ROOT.utopia_manifesto_recognized_external_partners value = THIS } } }
				ROOT = { add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_external_partners value = PREV } }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_fulfilled
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_first_external_case_complete
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_first_external_case_resolved
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_consensual_partner_proven
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_first_external_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.long_supply_contract }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_external_case_count } } set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_external_case_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_external_case_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_relief_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.long_supply_completion_relief }
		utopia_manifesto_relieve_long_supply_contract_deficit = yes
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_achievement_case_target_input = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_target_id }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_achievement_case_method_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.long_supply_contract }
		utopia_manifesto_record_achievement_peaceful_case_resolution = yes
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_step_loss }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_plenty_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_shift }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_step }
		utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_ledger_delta = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_long_supply_contract_runtime = yes
		utopia_manifesto_reconcile_league_reverse_links = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_dominant_case_family = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_case_candidate_targets = yes
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_league_state = yes
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_formation_proof = yes
	}
	else = { utopia_manifesto_fail_long_supply_contract = yes }
}

utopia_manifesto_fail_long_supply_contract = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_active }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_failed
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_relief_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.long_supply_failure_reversal }
		utopia_manifesto_relieve_long_supply_contract_deficit = yes
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_step }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_plenty_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_step_loss }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_shift_loss }
		utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_ledger_delta = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_long_supply_contract_runtime = yes
		utopia_manifesto_reconcile_league_reverse_links = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_dominant_case_family = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_case_candidate_targets = yes
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_league_state = yes
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_formation_proof = yes
	}
}

# State scope. ROOT is the exact Event 15 founder. Association charters outlive
# their proof mission, so they need durable founder, host, and state reverse
# links rather than relying on the temporary association-duties arrays.
utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_association_charter_founder = {
	save_event_target_as = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state_scope
	if = {
		limit = { owner = { exists = yes } }
		owner = { save_event_target_as = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_host_scope }
		# One state has one live owner. If ownership changed before the bounded
		# reconciler ran, remove the previous generation before registering a
		# founder under the new host; otherwise independent founder and host sets
		# could form a false cross-product.
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_founders^num
					value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero
					compare = greater_than
				}
				NOT = {
					is_in_array = {
						array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_hosts
						value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_association_charter_host_scope
					}
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = utopia_manifesto_changed_case_state
			clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_replaced_association_charter_founder_snapshot
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_founders
				add_to_temp_array = { utopia_manifesto_replaced_association_charter_founder_snapshot = THIS }
			}
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				array = utopia_manifesto_replaced_association_charter_founder_snapshot
				country_event = { id = chaosx.nr15.165 }
			}
			clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_replaced_association_charter_founder_snapshot
		}
		# Repair a stale host reverse link defensively before installing a new
		# founder. Normal unregister paths already keep this combination empty.
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_founders^num
					value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero
					compare = equals
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_hosts^num
					value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero
					compare = greater_than
				}
			}
			utopia_manifesto_clear_all_association_charter_links_from_state = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				is_in_array = {
					array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_founders
					value = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_founders value = ROOT }
	}
	ROOT = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					is_in_array = {
						array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state_targets
						value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state_scope
					}
				}
			}
			add_to_array = {
				array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state_targets
				value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state_scope
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { owner = { exists = yes } }
		owner = {
			save_event_target_as = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_host_scope
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_host
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						is_in_array = {
							array = utopia_manifesto_association_chartered_states
							value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state_scope
						}
					}
				}
				add_to_array = {
					array = utopia_manifesto_association_chartered_states
					value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state_scope
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					is_in_array = {
						array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_hosts
						value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_association_charter_host_scope
					}
				}
			}
			add_to_array = {
				array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_hosts
				value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_association_charter_host_scope
			}
		}
	}
}

# State scope. ROOT is the exact founder whose charter link is leaving. The
# visible state package is removed only after the final founder unregisters.
utopia_manifesto_unregister_root_as_association_charter_founder = {
	save_event_target_as = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state_scope
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_in_array = {
				array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_founders
				value = ROOT
			}
		}
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_founders value = ROOT }
	}
	ROOT = {
		remove_from_array = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state_targets
			value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state_scope
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_founders^num
				value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_hosts
			remove_from_array = {
				array = utopia_manifesto_association_chartered_states
				value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state_scope
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = utopia_manifesto_association_chartered_states^num
						value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero
						compare = equals
					}
				}
				clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_host
				clear_array = utopia_manifesto_association_chartered_states
			}
		}
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_founders
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_hosts
		clr_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state
		if = {
			limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state } }
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state }
		}
		clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_state_association_resources
		clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_state_association_resistance
	}
}

# State scope. Used when the host withdraws from every association. This
# releases every founder and host index before removing the visible charter.
utopia_manifesto_clear_all_association_charter_links_from_state = {
	save_event_target_as = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state_scope
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_founders
		remove_from_array = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state_targets
			value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state_scope
		}
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_hosts
		remove_from_array = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_association_chartered_states
			value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state_scope
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = utopia_manifesto_association_chartered_states^num
					value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_host
			clear_array = utopia_manifesto_association_chartered_states
		}
	}
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_founders
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_hosts
	clr_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state }
	}
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_state_association_resources
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_state_association_resistance
}

# Founder scope. Snapshotting avoids mutating the persistent state array while
# it is being iterated during terminal cleanup.
utopia_manifesto_clear_all_association_charter_links = {
	clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state_snapshot
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state_targets
		add_to_temp_array = { utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state_snapshot = THIS }
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state_snapshot
		utopia_manifesto_unregister_root_as_association_charter_founder = yes
	}
	clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state_snapshot
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state_targets
}

utopia_manifesto_remove_association_duties_relations = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_targets
		utopia_manifesto_remove_root_association_diplomacy = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_clear_association_duties_charter = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_states
		utopia_manifesto_unregister_root_as_association_charter_founder = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_register_root_association_duties_target = {
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_target
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_founders value = ROOT } } }
		add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_founders value = ROOT }
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_unregister_root_association_duties_target = {
	remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_founders value = ROOT }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_founders^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals } }
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_target
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_founders
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_clear_association_duties_tracking = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_targets
		utopia_manifesto_unregister_root_association_duties_target = yes
	}
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_targets
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_states
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_active
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_target_id
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_state_id
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_family
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_type
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_days
}

utopia_manifesto_cleanup_association_duties = {
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_cleanup_in_progress
	if = {
		limit = { has_active_mission = mission_utopia_prove_association_duties }
		remove_mission = mission_utopia_prove_association_duties
	}
	utopia_manifesto_remove_association_duties_relations = yes
	utopia_manifesto_clear_association_duties_charter = yes
	utopia_manifesto_clear_association_duties_tracking = yes
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_cleanup_in_progress
}

utopia_manifesto_start_association_duties_from_active_case = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			utopia_manifesto_active_need_case_is_valid = yes
			check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.association }
			NOT = { utopia_manifesto_has_active_external_case_term = yes }
		}
		utopia_manifesto_cleanup_association_duties = yes
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_active
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_association_duties_target_id = utopia_manifesto_case_target_id }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_association_duties_state_id = utopia_manifesto_case_state_id }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_association_duties_family = utopia_manifesto_case_family }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_association_duties_type = utopia_manifesto_case_type }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_targets
			utopia_manifesto_register_root_association_duties_target = yes
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = utopia_manifesto_opinion_partnership }
			ROOT = { add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_targets value = PREV } }
		}
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_states
			set_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state
			set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_state_association_resources = constant:utopia_manifesto_state_modifier_value.association_resources }
			set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_state_association_resistance = constant:utopia_manifesto_state_modifier_value.association_resistance }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state }
			ROOT = { add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_states value = PREV } }
			utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_association_charter_founder = yes
		}
		utopia_manifesto_apply_association_settlement = yes
		utopia_manifesto_reconcile_league_reverse_links = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_association_duties_duration = yes
		activate_mission = mission_utopia_prove_association_duties
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_association_duties_relief_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.association_initial_relief }
		utopia_manifesto_relieve_association_duties_deficit = yes
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_nudge_loss }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_plenty_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_nudge }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_step }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_assignment_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_nudge_loss }
		utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_ledger_delta = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_active_need_case = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_selected_country_target = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_case_candidate_targets = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_clear_association_review_runtime = {
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_review_pending
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_review_waiting_for_slot
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_review_confirmed
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_review_open }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_review_invalidated
	}
	else = { clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_review_invalidated }
}

# Target scope. A non-reusable reservation belongs to the delayed bridge until
# that bridge actually fires, even if the underlying association ends. A later
# association waits for the stale bridge to release its slot, preventing any
# delayed or open review from being mistaken for a new relationship generation.
utopia_manifesto_schedule_association_review = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_associate_partner
			NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_review_pending }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_review_open }
			check_variable = {
				var = utopia_manifesto_association_founders^num
				value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_review_slot_reserved } }
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_review_slot_reserved
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_review_pending
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_review_waiting_for_slot
			set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_association_review_days = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_duration.association_duties }
			meta_effect = {
				text = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr15.207 days = [DAYS] } }
				DAYS = "[?utopia_manifesto_association_review_days|.0]"
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_review_waiting_for_slot
		}
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_complete_association_duties = {
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_association_duties_is_valid = yes }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_targets
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_associate_partner
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_association_founders value = ROOT } } }
				add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_association_founders value = ROOT }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = ROOT.utopia_manifesto_recognized_associates value = THIS } } }
				ROOT = { add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_associates value = PREV } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = ROOT.utopia_manifesto_recognized_external_partners value = THIS } } }
				ROOT = { add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_external_partners value = PREV } }
			}
			utopia_manifesto_schedule_association_review = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_fulfilled
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_first_associate_recognized
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_first_external_case_complete
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_first_external_case_resolved
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_consensual_partner_proven
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_partner_autonomy_retained
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_first_external_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.association }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_external_case_count } } set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_external_case_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_external_case_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_association_duties_relief_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.association_completion_relief }
		utopia_manifesto_relieve_association_duties_deficit = yes
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_achievement_case_target_input = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_target_id }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_achievement_case_method_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.association }
		utopia_manifesto_record_achievement_peaceful_case_resolution = yes
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_step_loss }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_shift }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_assignment_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_shift_loss }
		utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_ledger_delta = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_association_duties_tracking = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_dominant_case_family = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_case_candidate_targets = yes
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_league_state = yes
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_formation_proof = yes
	}
	else = { utopia_manifesto_fail_association_duties = yes }
}

utopia_manifesto_fail_association_duties = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_active }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_failed
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_association_duties_relief_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.association_failure_reversal }
		utopia_manifesto_relieve_association_duties_deficit = yes
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_step }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_plenty_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_nudge_loss }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_shift_loss }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_assignment_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_nudge }
		utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_ledger_delta = yes
		utopia_manifesto_remove_association_duties_relations = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_association_duties_charter = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_association_duties_tracking = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_dominant_case_family = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_case_candidate_targets = yes
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_league_state = yes
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_formation_proof = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_withdraw_from_association = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_associate_partner }
		utopia_manifesto_clear_association_review_runtime = yes
		save_event_target_as = utopia_manifesto_recognition_partner_scope
		clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_withdrawing_association_founder_snapshot
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_association_founders
			add_to_temp_array = { utopia_manifesto_withdrawing_association_founder_snapshot = THIS }
		}
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_withdrawing_association_founder_snapshot
			save_event_target_as = utopia_manifesto_withdrawing_association_founder
			save_event_target_as = utopia_manifesto_recognition_founder_scope
			save_event_target_as = utopia_manifesto_diplomacy_founder_scope
			ROOT = { save_event_target_as = utopia_manifesto_diplomacy_partner_scope }
			utopia_manifesto_remove_saved_pair_association_diplomacy = yes
			ROOT = {
				remove_from_array = {
					array = utopia_manifesto_association_founders
					value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_withdrawing_association_founder
				}
			}
			remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_associates value = ROOT }
			utopia_manifesto_remove_saved_pair_recognition_if_unbased = yes
			set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_nudge }
			set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_shift_loss }
			set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_assignment_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_nudge }
			utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_ledger_delta = yes
			utopia_manifesto_refresh_league_state = yes
			utopia_manifesto_refresh_formation_proof = yes
		}
		clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_withdrawing_association_founder_snapshot
		clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_withdrawn_association_charter_state_snapshot
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_association_chartered_states
			add_to_temp_array = { utopia_manifesto_withdrawn_association_charter_state_snapshot = THIS }
		}
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_withdrawn_association_charter_state_snapshot
			utopia_manifesto_clear_all_association_charter_links_from_state = yes
		}
		clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_withdrawn_association_charter_state_snapshot
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_association_chartered_states
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_association_founders
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_association_created_access_founders
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_association_created_guarantee_founders
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_associate_partner
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_host
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_access_created
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_guarantee_created
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_withdrawn
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_continue_association = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_associate_partner }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_association_founders
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = utopia_manifesto_opinion_partnership }
			ROOT = { add_opinion_modifier = { target = PREV modifier = utopia_manifesto_opinion_partnership } }
			set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_nudge }
			set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_assignment_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_nudge_loss }
			utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_ledger_delta = yes
			utopia_manifesto_refresh_league_state = yes
			utopia_manifesto_refresh_formation_proof = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_review_confirmed
		utopia_manifesto_schedule_association_review = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_record_case_counteroffer = {
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_active_need_case_is_valid = yes }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_counteroffer_recorded
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_case_offer_input } } set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_offer_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.offer_required } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_offers = utopia_manifesto_case_offer_input }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_integrity = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.counteroffer_integrity_gain }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_stage = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_stage.offer }
		clamp_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_case_integrity min = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.integrity_minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.integrity_maximum }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_offer_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.offer_required }
}

utopia_manifesto_start_stewardship_from_active_case = {
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_active_need_case_is_valid = yes }
		utopia_manifesto_reset_current_stewardship_progress = yes
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_active
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_failed
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_proven
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_stage = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_stage.stewardship }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_stewardship_checks_passed = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_stewardship_provisions = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_stewardship_votes = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = mission_utopia_need_case_expiry }
			remove_mission = mission_utopia_need_case_expiry
		}
		utopia_manifesto_apply_active_case_state_baseline = yes
		utopia_manifesto_begin_stewardship_burden = yes
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_deficit_relief_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.stewardship_deficit_relief }
		utopia_manifesto_relieve_active_case_deficit = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_accept_case_settlement = {
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_active_need_case_is_valid = yes }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_settlement_accepted
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.purchase } }
			utopia_manifesto_apply_purchase_settlement = yes
			utopia_manifesto_start_stewardship_from_active_case = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.long_supply_contract } }
			utopia_manifesto_start_long_supply_contract_from_active_case = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.lease } }
			utopia_manifesto_apply_lease_settlement = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_type = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_type.island_project_lease } }
				utopia_manifesto_start_island_project_lease_from_active_case = yes
			}
			else = {
				utopia_manifesto_start_stewardship_from_active_case = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.settlement_agreement } }
			utopia_manifesto_start_settlement_agreement_from_active_case = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.joint_administration } }
			utopia_manifesto_apply_joint_administration_settlement = yes
			utopia_manifesto_start_stewardship_from_active_case = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.association } }
			utopia_manifesto_start_association_duties_from_active_case = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.ultimatum } }
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_coercive_conduct_used
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_conduct_ultimatum_settlement
			utopia_manifesto_apply_purchase_settlement = yes
			utopia_manifesto_start_stewardship_from_active_case = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_need_case_active
			utopia_manifesto_case_method_requires_state_transfer = yes
			NOT = { utopia_manifesto_active_case_target_can_survive_state_transfer = yes }
			NOT = { utopia_manifesto_active_case_has_resolved_state_possession = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_transfer_cancelled_to_preserve_target
		utopia_manifesto_invalidate_active_need_case = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_refuse_case_settlement = {
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_active_need_case_is_valid = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.ultimatum } }
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_ultimatum_refused
		}
		else = { set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_settlement_refused }
		subtract_from_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_local_support = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.refusal_support_loss }
		clamp_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_case_local_support min = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.local_support_minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.local_support_maximum }
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_issue_case_ultimatum = {
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_active_need_case_is_valid = yes }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.ultimatum }
		utopia_manifesto_record_case_ladder_skip = yes
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_stage = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_stage.offer }
		subtract_from_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_integrity = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.ultimatum_integrity_loss }
		subtract_from_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_local_support = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.ultimatum_support_loss }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_ultimatum_issued
		clamp_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_case_integrity min = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.integrity_minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.integrity_maximum }
		clamp_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_case_local_support min = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.local_support_minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.local_support_maximum }
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_enforce_active_need_case = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			utopia_manifesto_active_need_case_is_valid = yes
			utopia_manifesto_active_case_target_can_survive_state_transfer = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_wargoal_granted }
		}
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.unilateral_enforcement }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = utopia_manifesto_case_integrity
					value = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.stewardship_minimum_integrity
					compare = less_than
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unjustified_enforced_case
		}
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_targets
			save_event_target_as = utopia_manifesto_case_wargoal_target
			ROOT = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_wargoal_against = {
								target = event_target:utopia_manifesto_case_wargoal_target
								type = utopia_manifesto_necessary_ground_take_state
							}
						}
					}
					meta_effect = {
						text = {
							create_wargoal = {
								type = utopia_manifesto_necessary_ground_take_state
								target = event_target:utopia_manifesto_case_wargoal_target
								generator = { [UTOPIA_MANIFESTO_CASE_STATE_ID] }
							}
					}
						UTOPIA_MANIFESTO_CASE_STATE_ID = "[?utopia_manifesto_case_state_id|.0]"
					}
				}
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_enforced
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_wargoal_granted
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_remove_active_case_wargoal = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_wargoal_granted }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_targets
			ROOT = {
				remove_wargoal = {
					type = utopia_manifesto_necessary_ground_take_state
					target = PREV
				}
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_wargoal_granted
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_mark_enforcement_war_active = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_enforced
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_need_case_active
			FROM = { utopia_manifesto_is_exact_active_case_target_for_root = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_enforcement_war_active
		if = { limit = { has_active_mission = mission_utopia_need_case_expiry } remove_mission = mission_utopia_need_case_expiry }
		utopia_manifesto_record_enforcement_war_milestone = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_resolve_enforcement_after_peace = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_enforcement_war_active
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_need_case_active
			NOT = { utopia_manifesto_active_need_case_header_is_valid = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_target_disappeared
		utopia_manifesto_invalidate_active_need_case = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_enforcement_war_active
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_need_case_active
			any_of_scopes = {
				array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_targets
				NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_of_scopes = {
					array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_states
					is_owned_by = ROOT
				}
			}
			utopia_manifesto_start_stewardship_from_active_case = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_enforcement_failed
			set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_nudge }
			set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_plenty_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_nudge_loss }
			set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_shift_loss }
			set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_assignment_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_step }
			utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_ledger_delta = yes
			utopia_manifesto_clear_active_need_case = yes
			utopia_manifesto_clear_selected_country_target = yes
			utopia_manifesto_prepare_dominant_case_family = yes
			utopia_manifesto_prepare_case_candidate_targets = yes
		}
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_renounce_active_need_case = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_need_case_active }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_first_renounced_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.renunciation }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_renounced
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_false_cases_resolved
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_active }
			utopia_manifesto_restore_active_case_state_to_target = yes
			utopia_manifesto_resolve_stewardship_burden = yes
		}
		utopia_manifesto_remove_active_case_wargoal = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_active_need_case = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_stewardship_runtime = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_selected_country_target = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_dominant_case_family = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_case_candidate_targets = yes
	}
}

# Suspends a pre-stewardship case without erasing its exact target, state,
# family, method, integrity, or local-support record. Enforcement disappears
# immediately; the retained record exists only for the three public review
# outcomes and cannot pass the ordinary action gate.
utopia_manifesto_begin_obsolete_case_review = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_need_case_active
			NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_obsolete_case_review_pending }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_enforcement_war_active }
			check_variable = {
				var = utopia_manifesto_case_stage
				value = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_stage.stewardship
				compare = less_than
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_obsolete_case_review_pending
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_expired
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = mission_utopia_need_case_expiry }
			remove_mission = mission_utopia_need_case_expiry
		}
		utopia_manifesto_clear_case_response_state = yes
		utopia_manifesto_remove_active_case_wargoal = yes
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_ultimatum_issued
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_enforced
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_wargoal_granted
	}
}

# The case may continue only when its original calling deficit and selected
# target/state remain live. It returns to the peaceful ladder with a short
# public deadline; an old ultimatum or private wargoal is never restored.
utopia_manifesto_continue_obsolete_case_at_political_cost = {
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_obsolete_case_can_continue = yes }
		utopia_manifesto_clear_case_response_state = yes
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_obsolete_case_review_pending
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_obsolete_case_review_due_to_deficit
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_obsolete_case_review_due_to_expiry
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_expired
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_continued_after_review
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_conduct_obsolete_case_continued
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_achievement_disqualifier_stale_claim
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.unset }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_case_state_id }
			set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_stage = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_stage.offer }
		}
		else = { set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_stage = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_stage.claim } }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_expiry_days = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.mature_limited_expiry_days }
		subtract_from_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_integrity = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.peaceful_ladder_skip_integrity_loss }
		subtract_from_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_local_support = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.refusal_support_loss }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = utopia_manifesto_case_integrity
			min = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.integrity_minimum
			max = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.integrity_maximum
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = utopia_manifesto_case_local_support
			min = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.local_support_minimum
			max = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.local_support_maximum
		}
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_shift_loss }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_assignment_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_step }
		utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_ledger_delta = yes
		activate_mission = mission_utopia_need_case_expiry
	}
}

# Converts the suspended claim into one bounded bilateral offer. Resource and
# port cases become long-supply contracts, settlement/reconstruction cases use
# a settlement agreement, and defensive/island cases use a lease. None of the
# three paths grants ownership, a core, or an automatic wargoal.
utopia_manifesto_offer_obsolete_case_limited_treaty = {
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_obsolete_case_can_convert_to_limited_treaty = yes }
		utopia_manifesto_clear_case_response_state = yes
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_obsolete_case_review_pending
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_obsolete_case_treaty_pending
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_response_active
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_type = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_type.port_access }
					check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_type = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_type.essential_resource }
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_offer_long_supply
			set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.long_supply_contract }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_type = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_type.settlement_and_housing }
					check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_type = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_type.reconstruction_zone }
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_offer_settlement_agreement
			set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.settlement_agreement }
		}
		else = {
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_offer_lease
			set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.lease }
		}
		utopia_manifesto_record_prepared_case_method = yes
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_offer_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_value.standard_offer }
		# This is a corrective treaty for an already-reviewed case, not a fresh
		# attempt to jump the peaceful ladder. Keep the method attempt record while
		# suppressing the shortcut penalty.
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_ladder_restoration_in_progress
		utopia_manifesto_record_case_offer = yes
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_ladder_restoration_in_progress
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_case_decision_duration = yes
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_targets
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_obsolete_case_treaty_offered
			ROOT = {
				activate_targeted_decision = {
					target = PREV
					decision = mission_utopia_wait_for_need_answer
				}
			}
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr15.219 }
		}
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_accept_obsolete_case_limited_treaty = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_obsolete_case_treaty_pending
			utopia_manifesto_active_need_case_is_valid = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_converted_to_treaty
		utopia_manifesto_accept_case_settlement = yes
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_obsolete_case_treaty_pending
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_obsolete_case_review_due_to_deficit
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_obsolete_case_review_due_to_expiry
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_response_active
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_step }
		utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_ledger_delta = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_close_obsolete_case_after_treaty_refusal = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_need_case_active }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_limited_treaty_refused
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_renounced_when_need_ended
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_closed_for_domestic_alternative
		utopia_manifesto_record_achievement_obsolete_case_renunciation = yes
		utopia_manifesto_record_achievement_peaceful_case_resolution = yes
		utopia_manifesto_renounce_active_need_case = yes
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_nudge }
		utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_ledger_delta = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_expire_active_need_case = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_need_case_active }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_expired
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_active }
			utopia_manifesto_restore_active_case_state_to_target = yes
			utopia_manifesto_resolve_stewardship_burden = yes
		}
		utopia_manifesto_remove_active_case_wargoal = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_active_need_case = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_stewardship_runtime = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_selected_country_target = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_dominant_case_family = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_case_candidate_targets = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_adopt_annexer_as_stewardship_successor = {
	utopia_manifesto_clear_active_case_created_diplomacy = yes
	utopia_manifesto_clear_active_case_target_links = yes
	utopia_manifesto_clear_selected_country_target = yes
	event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexing_country = {
		ROOT = {
			set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_target_id = PREV.id }
			add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_targets value = PREV }
			set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_selected_country_id = PREV.id }
			add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_selected_country_targets value = PREV }
		}
		utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_active_case_founder = yes
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_selected_country_target
	}
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_target_succeeded_by_annexer
	subtract_from_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_integrity = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.target_succession_integrity_loss }
	subtract_from_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_local_support = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.target_succession_support_loss }
	clamp_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_case_integrity min = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.integrity_minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.integrity_maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_case_local_support min = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.local_support_minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.local_support_maximum }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_nudge_loss }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_assignment_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_nudge }
	utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_ledger_delta = yes
	utopia_manifesto_validate_active_need_case = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_close_case_after_founder_extinguishes_target = {
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_target_extinguished_by_founder
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_coercive_conduct_used
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unresolved_stewardship_failure
	utopia_manifesto_record_achievement_unrelated_case_annexation = yes
	utopia_manifesto_clear_active_case_created_diplomacy = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_active }
		utopia_manifesto_resolve_stewardship_burden = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_stewardship_runtime = yes
	}
	utopia_manifesto_clear_active_need_case = yes
	utopia_manifesto_clear_selected_country_target = yes
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_nudge }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_shift_loss }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_assignment_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_step }
	utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_ledger_delta = yes
	utopia_manifesto_prepare_dominant_case_family = yes
	utopia_manifesto_prepare_case_candidate_targets = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_handle_active_case_target_annexation = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_need_case_active
			has_variable = utopia_manifesto_case_target_id
			event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_case_target = {
				check_variable = {
					var = id
					value = ROOT.utopia_manifesto_case_target_id
					compare = equals
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_active
				event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexing_country = {
					exists = yes
					NOT = { tag = ROOT }
				}
			}
			utopia_manifesto_adopt_annexer_as_stewardship_successor = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexing_country = { tag = ROOT }
			}
			utopia_manifesto_close_case_after_founder_extinguishes_target = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_target_disappeared
			utopia_manifesto_invalidate_active_need_case = yes
		}
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_validate_active_need_case = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_need_case_active
			NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_obsolete_case_review_pending }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_obsolete_case_treaty_pending }
			NOT = { utopia_manifesto_active_need_case_is_valid = yes }
		}
		utopia_manifesto_invalidate_active_need_case = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_invalidate_active_need_case = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_need_case_active }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_invalidated
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_active }
			utopia_manifesto_fail_invalid_stewardship_case = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { utopia_manifesto_has_ledger = yes }
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_obsolete_case_review_due_to_deficit
			utopia_manifesto_begin_obsolete_case_review = yes
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr15.54 }
		}
		else = {
			utopia_manifesto_expire_active_need_case = yes
		}
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_fail_invalid_stewardship_case = {
	utopia_manifesto_trigger_stewardship_revolt = yes
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Stewardship proof and outcomes
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

utopia_manifesto_record_stewardship_provision = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_active }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_provision_input } } set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_stewardship_provision_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_stewardship_provisions = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_provision_input }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_stewardship_checks_passed = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_provision_input }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_integrity = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.provision_integrity_gain }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_local_support = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.provision_support_gain }
		clamp_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_case_integrity min = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.integrity_minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.integrity_maximum }
		clamp_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_case_local_support min = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.local_support_minimum max = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.local_support_maximum }
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_stewardship_proof = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_record_stewardship_charter = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_active }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_charter_recorded } }
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_charter_recorded
			add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_stewardship_checks_passed = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one }
		}
		utopia_manifesto_apply_local_charter_to_active_case_state = yes
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_stewardship_proof = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_record_stewardship_vote = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_active }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_vote_support_input } } set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_stewardship_vote_support_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_stewardship_votes = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_vote_support_input }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_stewardship_vote_support_input > constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero } }
			add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_stewardship_checks_passed = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one }
		}
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_stewardship_proof = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_refresh_stewardship_proof = {
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_stewardship_proof_met = yes }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_proven
	}
	else = {
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_proven
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_record_external_case_completion = {
	utopia_manifesto_record_achievement_peaceful_case_resolution = yes
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_first_external_case_complete
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_first_external_case_resolved
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_first_external_case_method = utopia_manifesto_case_method }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.association } }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_first_associate_recognized
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_consensual_partner_proven
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_targets
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = ROOT.utopia_manifesto_recognized_associates value = THIS } } }
				ROOT = { add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_associates value = PREV } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = ROOT.utopia_manifesto_recognized_external_partners value = THIS } } }
				ROOT = { add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_external_partners value = PREV } }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.purchase }
				check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.long_supply_contract }
				check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.lease }
				check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.joint_administration }
				check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.settlement_agreement }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_consensual_partner_proven
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_targets
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { ROOT.utopia_manifesto_case_type = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_type.island_project_lease } }
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_compact_founders value = ROOT } } }
					add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_compact_founders value = ROOT }
				}
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_compact_founders value = ROOT } } }
					add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_compact_founders value = ROOT }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = ROOT.utopia_manifesto_recognized_compacts value = THIS } } }
				ROOT = { add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_compacts value = PREV } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = ROOT.utopia_manifesto_recognized_external_partners value = THIS } } }
				ROOT = { add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_external_partners value = PREV } }
			}
		}
	}
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_external_case_count } } set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_external_case_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero } }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_external_case_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one }
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_league_state = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_clear_temporary_stewardship_state_modifiers = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_states
		clr_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_obligation_state
		clr_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_lease_administration_state
		clr_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_joint_administration_state
		clr_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_provisioned_stewardship_state
		clr_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_restored_stewardship_route_state
		clr_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_local_charter_state
		clr_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_assigned_colony_state
		if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_obligation_state } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_obligation_state } }
		if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_lease_administration_state } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_lease_administration_state } }
		if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_joint_administration_state } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_joint_administration_state } }
		if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_provisioned_stewardship_state } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_provisioned_stewardship_state } }
		if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_restored_stewardship_route_state } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_restored_stewardship_route_state } }
		if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_local_charter_state } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_local_charter_state } }
		if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_assigned_colony_state } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_assigned_colony_state } }
		clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_state_stewardship_supply
		clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_state_stewardship_resistance
		clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_state_lease_supply
		clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_state_lease_resources
		clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_state_joint_supply
		clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_state_joint_construction
		clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_state_provision_supply
		clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_state_route_construction
