		clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_state_charter_resources
		clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_state_charter_resistance
		clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_state_assigned_supply
		clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_state_assigned_resistance
	}
}

# Founder scope. This tears down only relations the exact active case created;
# it does not move a state. Integration and return can therefore share the
# same provenance-safe diplomatic cleanup without conflating their outcomes.
utopia_manifesto_clear_active_case_created_diplomacy = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_targets
		if = {
			limit = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_created_access_founders value = ROOT } }
			remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_created_access_founders value = ROOT }
			if = {
				limit = {
					gives_military_access_to = ROOT
					NOT = { utopia_manifesto_has_event15_access_creator_for_root = yes }
				}
				diplomatic_relation = { country = ROOT relation = military_access active = no }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_created_guarantee_founders value = ROOT } }
			remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_case_created_guarantee_founders value = ROOT }
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_guaranteed_by = ROOT
					NOT = { utopia_manifesto_has_event15_founder_guarantee_creator_for_root = yes }
				}
				ROOT = { diplomatic_relation = { country = PREV relation = guarantee active = no } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_case_created_access_founders^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals } }
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_created_access_to_founder
			clear_array = utopia_manifesto_case_created_access_founders
		}
	}
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_created_guarantee_to_target
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_created_reciprocal_access }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_targets
			if = {
				limit = { ROOT = { gives_military_access_to = PREV } }
				ROOT = { diplomatic_relation = { country = PREV relation = military_access active = no } }
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_created_reciprocal_access
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_restore_active_case_state_to_target = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_targets
		save_event_target_as = utopia_manifesto_case_return_target
	}
	utopia_manifesto_clear_active_case_created_diplomacy = yes
	utopia_manifesto_clear_temporary_stewardship_state_modifiers = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = utopia_manifesto_case_return_target }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_states
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { ROOT.utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.purchase }
						check_variable = { ROOT.utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.ultimatum }
						check_variable = { ROOT.utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.unilateral_enforcement }
					}
					is_owned_by = ROOT
				}
				transfer_state_to = event_target:utopia_manifesto_case_return_target
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { ROOT.utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.lease }
						check_variable = { ROOT.utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.joint_administration }
					}
					owner = { utopia_manifesto_is_exact_active_case_target_for_root = yes }
				}
				set_state_controller_to = event_target:utopia_manifesto_case_return_target
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { NOT = { owner = { utopia_manifesto_is_exact_active_case_target_for_root = yes } } }
				ROOT = { set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_ownership_reconciliation_required }
			}
			clr_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_purchase_settlement_state
		}
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_apply_provision_to_active_case_state = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_states
		set_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_provisioned_stewardship_state
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_state_provision_supply = constant:utopia_manifesto_state_modifier_value.provision_supply }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_provisioned_stewardship_state }
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_apply_route_restoration_to_active_case_state = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_states
		set_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_restored_stewardship_route_state
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_state_route_construction = constant:utopia_manifesto_state_modifier_value.route_construction }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_restored_stewardship_route_state }
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_apply_local_charter_to_active_case_state = {

		array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_states
		set_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_local_charter_state

		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_local_charter_state }
	}

utopia_manifesto_apply_assigned_colony_to_active_case_state = {

		array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_states
		set_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_assigned_colony_state
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_state_assigned_supply = constant:utopia_manifesto_state_modifier_value.assigned_supply }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_state_assigned_resistance = constant:utopia_manifesto_state_modifier_value.assigned_resistance }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_assigned_colony_state }
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_finalize_stewardship_association_vote = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_associate_partnership
			utopia_manifesto_stewardship_vote_association_is_available = yes
		}
		# Restore by the original acquisition method before the public record is
		# changed to association.
		utopia_manifesto_restore_active_case_state_to_target = yes
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.association }
		utopia_manifesto_record_external_case_completion = yes
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_targets
			utopia_manifesto_apply_root_association_diplomacy = yes
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_associate_partner
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_association_founders value = ROOT } } }
				add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_association_founders value = ROOT }
			}
			utopia_manifesto_schedule_association_review = yes
		}
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_states
			set_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state
			set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_state_association_resources = constant:utopia_manifesto_state_modifier_value.association_resources }
			set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_state_association_resistance = constant:utopia_manifesto_state_modifier_value.association_resistance }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state }
			utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_association_charter_founder = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_association_completed
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_partner_autonomy_retained
		utopia_manifesto_resolve_stewardship_burden = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_stewardship_runtime = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_active_need_case = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_selected_country_target = yes
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_nudge_loss }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_plenty_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_nudge }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_shift }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_assignment_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_shift_loss }
		utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_ledger_delta = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_dominant_case_family = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_case_candidate_targets = yes
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_league_state = yes
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_formation_proof = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_finalize_stewardship_autonomy_vote = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_autonomy_selected
			utopia_manifesto_stewardship_vote_autonomy_is_available = yes
		}
		utopia_manifesto_restore_active_case_state_to_target = yes
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.autonomy_pact }
		utopia_manifesto_record_external_case_completion = yes
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_targets
			utopia_manifesto_apply_root_stewardship_autonomy_pact = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_autonomy_pact_completed
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_partner_autonomy_retained
		utopia_manifesto_resolve_stewardship_burden = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_stewardship_runtime = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_active_need_case = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_selected_country_target = yes
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_nudge_loss }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_plenty_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_value.none }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_shift }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_assignment_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_step_loss }
		utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_ledger_delta = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_dominant_case_family = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_case_candidate_targets = yes
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_league_state = yes
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_formation_proof = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_finalize_stewardship_return_vote = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_active }
		utopia_manifesto_restore_active_case_state_to_target = yes
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_method = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_method.renunciation }
		utopia_manifesto_record_external_case_completion = yes
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_returned
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_partner_autonomy_retained
		utopia_manifesto_resolve_stewardship_burden = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_stewardship_runtime = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_active_need_case = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_selected_country_target = yes
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_nudge }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_plenty_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_value.none }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_step }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_assignment_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_nudge_loss }
		utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_ledger_delta = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_dominant_case_family = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_case_candidate_targets = yes
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_league_state = yes
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_formation_proof = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_return_stewardship = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_active }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_partner_autonomy_retained
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_returned
		utopia_manifesto_record_external_case_completion = yes
		utopia_manifesto_restore_active_case_state_to_target = yes
		utopia_manifesto_resolve_stewardship_burden = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_stewardship_runtime = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_active_need_case = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_selected_country_target = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_dominant_case_family = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_case_candidate_targets = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_integrate_stewardship = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_active
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_proven
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_long_integration_complete
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_integration_selected
				utopia_manifesto_is_closed_island_route = yes
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_integration_authorized
		utopia_manifesto_record_external_case_completion = yes
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_case_deficit_relief_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_case_limits.completed_stewardship_relief }
		utopia_manifesto_relieve_active_case_deficit = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_active_case_created_diplomacy = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_temporary_stewardship_state_modifiers = yes
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_states
			transfer_state_to = ROOT
			clr_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_purchase_settlement_state
			set_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_integrated_commonwealth_state
			set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_state_integrated_resources = constant:utopia_manifesto_state_modifier_value.integrated_resources }
			set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_state_integrated_construction = constant:utopia_manifesto_state_modifier_value.integrated_construction }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_integrated_commonwealth_state }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = ROOT.utopia_manifesto_integrated_commonwealth_states value = THIS } } }
				ROOT = { add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_integrated_commonwealth_states value = PREV } }
			}
		}
		utopia_manifesto_complete_stewardship_settlement = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_stewardship_runtime = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_active_need_case = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_selected_country_target = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_dominant_case_family = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_case_candidate_targets = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_capture_stewardship_revolt_scopes = {
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_revolt_targets clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_revolt_target }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_revolt_states clr_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_revolt_state }
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_revolt_targets
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_revolt_states
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_targets
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_revolt_target
		ROOT = { add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_revolt_targets value = PREV } }
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_active_case_states
		set_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_revolt_state
		ROOT = { add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_revolt_states value = PREV } }
	}
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_revolt_pending
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_failed
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unresolved_stewardship_failure
}

utopia_manifesto_trigger_stewardship_revolt = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_active }
		utopia_manifesto_capture_stewardship_revolt_scopes = yes
		utopia_manifesto_record_assigned_colony_revolt_milestone = yes
		utopia_manifesto_restore_active_case_state_to_target = yes
		utopia_manifesto_resolve_stewardship_burden = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_stewardship_runtime = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_active_need_case = yes
		utopia_manifesto_clear_selected_country_target = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_dominant_case_family = yes
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_case_candidate_targets = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_clear_stewardship_revolt_runtime = {
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_revolt_targets clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_revolt_target }
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_revolt_states
		clr_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_revolt_state
		clr_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_assigned_colony_state
		if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_assigned_colony_state } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_assigned_colony_state } }
		clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_state_assigned_supply
		clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_state_assigned_resistance
	}
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_revolt_targets
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_revolt_states
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_revolt_pending
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_failed
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_unresolved_stewardship_failure
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_assigned_colony_active
}

utopia_manifesto_resolve_revolt_by_return = {
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_colony_independence_accepted
	utopia_manifesto_clear_stewardship_revolt_runtime = yes
	utopia_manifesto_clear_stewardship_idea_stages = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_resolve_revolt_by_association = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_revolt_targets
		utopia_manifesto_register_root_colony_association = yes
	}
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_colony_association_negotiated
	utopia_manifesto_clear_stewardship_revolt_runtime = yes
	utopia_manifesto_resolve_stewardship_burden = yes
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_league_state = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_resolve_revolt_by_assigned_administration = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_revolt_targets
		save_event_target_as = utopia_manifesto_revolt_administration_target
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_assigned_colony_target
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_revolt_states
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_event_target = utopia_manifesto_revolt_administration_target
				owner = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_revolt_target }
			}
			set_state_controller_to = ROOT
			set_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_assigned_colony_state
			set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_state_assigned_supply = constant:utopia_manifesto_state_modifier_value.assigned_supply }
			set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_state_assigned_resistance = constant:utopia_manifesto_state_modifier_value.assigned_resistance }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_assigned_colony_state }
		}
	}
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_assigned_colony_active
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_conduct_colony_repressed
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_achievement_disqualifier_colonial_repression
	utopia_manifesto_assign_colonial_administration = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_resolve_revolt_by_mediation = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_revolt_targets
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = utopia_manifesto_opinion_partnership }
	}
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_colony_foreign_mediation
	utopia_manifesto_clear_stewardship_revolt_runtime = yes
	utopia_manifesto_resolve_stewardship_burden = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_end_assigned_revolt_administration = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_revolt_targets
		save_event_target_as = utopia_manifesto_revolt_administration_target
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_assigned_colony_target
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_revolt_states
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_event_target = utopia_manifesto_revolt_administration_target
				owner = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_revolt_target }
			}
			set_state_controller_to = event_target:utopia_manifesto_revolt_administration_target
		}
		else = { ROOT = { set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_case_ownership_reconciliation_required } }
	}
	utopia_manifesto_clear_stewardship_revolt_runtime = yes
	utopia_manifesto_resolve_stewardship_burden = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_remove_active_stewardship_missions = {
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_cleanup_in_progress
	if = { limit = { has_active_mission = mission_utopia_emergency_provision } remove_mission = mission_utopia_emergency_provision }
	if = { limit = { has_active_mission = mission_utopia_restore_stewardship_route } remove_mission = mission_utopia_restore_stewardship_route }
	if = { limit = { has_active_mission = mission_utopia_hold_charter_period } remove_mission = mission_utopia_hold_charter_period }
	if = { limit = { has_active_mission = mission_utopia_long_integration } remove_mission = mission_utopia_long_integration }
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_provision_active
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_route_active
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_charter_period_active
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_integration_active
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_objective_active
}

utopia_manifesto_reset_current_stewardship_progress = {
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_obligation_confirmed
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_provision_complete
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_route_restored
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_charter_convened
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_charter_period_complete
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_status_vote_held
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_assigned_colony_active
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_integration_authorized
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_long_integration_complete
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_integration_selected
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_associate_partnership
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_autonomy_selected
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_return_selected
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_failed
}

utopia_manifesto_clear_stewardship_runtime = {
	utopia_manifesto_remove_active_stewardship_missions = yes
	utopia_manifesto_clear_temporary_stewardship_state_modifiers = yes
	utopia_manifesto_reset_current_stewardship_progress = yes
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_active
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_proven
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_charter_recorded
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_checks_passed
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_provisions
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_votes
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_cleanup_in_progress
}

utopia_manifesto_clear_integrated_commonwealth_state_runtime = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_integrated_commonwealth_states
		clr_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_integrated_commonwealth_state
		if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_integrated_commonwealth_state } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_integrated_commonwealth_state } }
		clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_state_integrated_resources
		clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_state_integrated_construction
	}
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_integrated_commonwealth_states
}

utopia_manifesto_reconcile_integrated_commonwealth_state_ownership = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_integrated_commonwealth_states
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_owned_by = ROOT } }
			clr_state_flag = utopia_manifesto_integrated_commonwealth_state
			if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_integrated_commonwealth_state } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = utopia_manifesto_integrated_commonwealth_state } }
			clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_state_integrated_resources
			clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_state_integrated_construction
			ROOT = { remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_integrated_commonwealth_states value = PREV } }
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# League membership, cohesion, aid, reserve, defense, and failure
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# Partner scope. ROOT remains the exact Event 15 founder. The partner-side
# reverse array lets one-shot annexation callbacks notify only founders that
# actually recorded a live League or formation-count relationship.
utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_league_founder = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				exists = yes
				has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_accepted
			}
			NOT = { tag = ROOT }
			utopia_manifesto_has_live_league_relation_for_root = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_linked_partner
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					is_in_array = {
						array = utopia_manifesto_league_founders
						value = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
			add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_founders value = ROOT }
		}
		ROOT = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						is_in_array = {
							array = utopia_manifesto_league_reverse_link_targets
							value = PREV
						}
					}
				}
				add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_reverse_link_targets value = PREV }
			}
		}
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_force_unregister_root_as_league_founder = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_in_array = {
				array = utopia_manifesto_league_founders
				value = ROOT
			}
		}
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_founders value = ROOT }
	}
	ROOT = {
		remove_from_array = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_league_reverse_link_targets
			value = PREV
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = utopia_manifesto_league_founders^num
				value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_linked_partner
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_founders
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_unregister_root_as_league_founder_if_unrelated = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { utopia_manifesto_has_live_league_relation_for_root = yes } }
		utopia_manifesto_force_unregister_root_as_league_founder = yes
	}
}

# Founder scope. Reconciliation is bounded to arrays already owned by this
# Event 15 actor. It both adopts live relations from saves made before the
# reverse-link contract and releases stale partner-side founder records.
utopia_manifesto_reconcile_league_reverse_links = {
	clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_league_reverse_link_snapshot
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_league_reverse_link_targets
		add_to_temp_array = { utopia_manifesto_league_reverse_link_snapshot = THIS }
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_league_reverse_link_snapshot
		utopia_manifesto_unregister_root_as_league_founder_if_unrelated = yes
	}
	clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_league_reverse_link_snapshot

	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_candidate_targets utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_league_founder = yes }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsor_targets utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_league_founder = yes }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_action_targets utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_league_founder = yes }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_technical_targets utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_league_founder = yes }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_reserve_pending_targets utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_league_founder = yes }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_invited_targets utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_league_founder = yes }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_pending_targets utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_league_founder = yes }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_pending_targets utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_league_founder = yes }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_targets utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_league_founder = yes }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_targets utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_league_founder = yes }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_targets utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_league_founder = yes }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lessor_targets utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_league_founder = yes }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_autonomy_partners utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_league_founder = yes }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_colony_association_partners utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_league_founder = yes }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_members utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_league_founder = yes }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_observers utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_league_founder = yes }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsors utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_league_founder = yes }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_aid_targets utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_league_founder = yes }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_reserve_targets utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_league_founder = yes }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_defense_targets utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_league_founder = yes }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_created_recognized_partners utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_league_founder = yes }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_external_partners utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_league_founder = yes }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_compacts utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_league_founder = yes }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_associates utopia_manifesto_register_root_as_league_founder = yes }
}

utopia_manifesto_clear_all_league_reverse_links = {
	clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_league_reverse_link_snapshot
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_league_reverse_link_targets
		add_to_temp_array = { utopia_manifesto_league_reverse_link_snapshot = THIS }
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_league_reverse_link_snapshot
		utopia_manifesto_force_unregister_root_as_league_founder = yes
	}
	clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_league_reverse_link_snapshot
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_reverse_link_targets
}

# Founder scope. The annexed partner is a regular event target saved by
# on_annex. The durable founder-side state index remains available even after
# terminal cleanup clears the annexed partner's own arrays.
utopia_manifesto_clear_association_charters_for_annexed_partner = {
	clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_annexed_association_charter_state_snapshot
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state_targets
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_in_array = {
						array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_founders
						value = ROOT
					}
					is_in_array = {
						array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_hosts
						value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner
					}
				}
				add_to_temp_array = { utopia_manifesto_annexed_association_charter_state_snapshot = THIS }
			}
		}
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_annexed_association_charter_state_snapshot
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_array = {
					array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_founders
					value = ROOT
				}
			}
			utopia_manifesto_unregister_root_as_association_charter_founder = yes
		}
	}
	clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_annexed_association_charter_state_snapshot
}

# Founder scope. The changed state is saved by on_state_control_changed. An
# occupation leaves the charter in place; a real owner change away from its
# recorded host releases only this founder's charter and external-network link.
utopia_manifesto_handle_changed_association_charter_state = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = utopia_manifesto_changed_case_state
			event_target:utopia_manifesto_changed_case_state = {
				is_in_array = {
					array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_founders
					value = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_active
				is_in_array = {
					array = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_states
					value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_changed_case_state
				}
				NOT = { utopia_manifesto_association_duties_is_valid = yes }
			}
			utopia_manifesto_fail_association_duties = yes
		}
		else = {
			event_target:utopia_manifesto_changed_case_state = {
				owner = { save_event_target_as = utopia_manifesto_changed_association_charter_owner }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_event_target = utopia_manifesto_changed_association_charter_owner
					event_target:utopia_manifesto_changed_case_state = {
						NOT = {
							is_in_array = {
								array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_hosts
								value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_changed_association_charter_owner
							}
						}
					}
				}
				clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_changed_association_charter_host_snapshot
				event_target:utopia_manifesto_changed_case_state = {
					for_each_scope_loop = {
						array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_hosts
						add_to_temp_array = { utopia_manifesto_changed_association_charter_host_snapshot = THIS }
					}
					utopia_manifesto_unregister_root_as_association_charter_founder = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_association_relation_lost = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
				for_each_scope_loop = {
					array = utopia_manifesto_changed_association_charter_host_snapshot
					save_event_target_as = utopia_manifesto_changed_association_charter_host
					set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_association_host_still_linked = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
					for_each_scope_loop = {
						array = utopia_manifesto_association_chartered_states
						if = {
							limit = {
								is_in_array = {
									array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_founders
									value = ROOT
								}
							}
							set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_association_host_still_linked = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = {
								var = utopia_manifesto_association_host_still_linked
								value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero
								compare = equals
							}
						}
						save_event_target_as = utopia_manifesto_recognition_partner_scope
						ROOT = { save_event_target_as = utopia_manifesto_recognition_founder_scope }
						remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_association_founders value = ROOT }
						ROOT = {
							remove_from_array = {
								array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_associates
								value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_changed_association_charter_host
							}
						}
						utopia_manifesto_remove_saved_pair_recognition_if_unbased = yes
						utopia_manifesto_remove_root_association_diplomacy = yes
						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = {
									var = utopia_manifesto_association_founders^num
									value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero
									compare = equals
								}
							}
							clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_associate_partner
							clear_array = utopia_manifesto_association_founders
							utopia_manifesto_clear_association_review_runtime = yes
						}
						set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_association_relation_lost = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one }
					}
				}
				clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_changed_association_charter_host_snapshot
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							var = utopia_manifesto_association_relation_lost
							value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one
							compare = equals
						}
					}
					set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_lost
					set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_nudge }
					set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_change.ledger_shift_loss }
					utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_ledger_delta = yes
					utopia_manifesto_refresh_league_state = yes
					utopia_manifesto_refresh_formation_proof = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Founder scope. The engine exposes controller changes but not a dedicated
# owner-change on_action. The existing actor-scoped Event 15 pulse therefore
# checks only this founder's recorded charter states, catching peace transfers
# where the new owner already controlled the state.
utopia_manifesto_reconcile_association_charter_ownership = {
	clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_owner_review_snapshot
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_state_targets
		add_to_temp_array = { utopia_manifesto_association_charter_owner_review_snapshot = THIS }
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_owner_review_snapshot
		save_event_target_as = utopia_manifesto_changed_case_state
		ROOT = { utopia_manifesto_handle_changed_association_charter_state = yes }
	}
	clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_association_charter_owner_review_snapshot
}

# Founder scope. Active bilateral terms are validated on the same bounded
# actor pulse, so annexation, war, or ownership changes cannot leave a dead
# mission/state package waiting until its nominal timeout.
utopia_manifesto_reconcile_external_term_integrity = {
	utopia_manifesto_reconcile_association_charter_ownership = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_active
			NOT = { utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_is_valid = yes }
		}
		utopia_manifesto_fail_settlement_agreement = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_active
			NOT = { utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_is_valid = yes }
		}
		utopia_manifesto_fail_long_supply_contract = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_active
			NOT = { utopia_manifesto_association_duties_is_valid = yes }
		}
		utopia_manifesto_fail_association_duties = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lease_active
			NOT = { utopia_manifesto_island_project_lease_is_valid = yes }
		}
		utopia_manifesto_expire_island_project_lease = yes
	}
}

# Founder scope. The annexed partner and annexer are regular event targets
# saved by on_annex before the target's reverse-founder arrays are cleared.
utopia_manifesto_handle_annexed_league_partner = {
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_relation_recorded = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_member_recorded = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_annexed_association_duties_recorded = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_annexed_settlement_agreement_recorded = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_annexed_long_supply_contract_recorded = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_annexed_island_lessor_recorded = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner
			event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner = {
				utopia_manifesto_has_recorded_league_relation_for_root = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_relation_recorded = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner
			is_in_array = {
				array = utopia_manifesto_league_members
				value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_member_recorded = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner
			is_in_array = {
				array = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_targets
				value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_annexed_association_duties_recorded = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner
			is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_targets value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_annexed_settlement_agreement_recorded = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner
			is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_targets value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_annexed_long_supply_contract_recorded = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner
			is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lessor_targets value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_annexed_island_lessor_recorded = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_relation_recorded
				value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = utopia_manifesto_annexed_association_duties_recorded
					value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_active }
				utopia_manifesto_fail_association_duties = yes
			}
			else = { utopia_manifesto_cleanup_association_duties = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_annexed_settlement_agreement_recorded = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one } }
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_active }
				utopia_manifesto_fail_settlement_agreement = yes
			}
			else = { utopia_manifesto_clear_settlement_agreement_runtime = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_annexed_long_supply_contract_recorded = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one } }
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_active }
				utopia_manifesto_fail_long_supply_contract = yes
			}
			else = { utopia_manifesto_clear_long_supply_contract_runtime = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_annexed_island_lessor_recorded = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one } }
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lease_active }
				utopia_manifesto_expire_island_project_lease = yes
			}
			else = { utopia_manifesto_return_island_project_lease = yes }
		}
		event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner = {
			utopia_manifesto_clear_root_island_lease_response = yes
			utopia_manifesto_clear_root_island_lease_renewal_reservation = yes
		}
		event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_autonomy_founders value = ROOT } }
				utopia_manifesto_remove_root_stewardship_autonomy_pact = yes
			}
			utopia_manifesto_remove_root_non_autonomy_compact_sources = yes
			if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_colony_association_founders value = ROOT } }
				utopia_manifesto_remove_root_colony_association = yes
			}
			utopia_manifesto_remove_root_association_diplomacy = yes
			utopia_manifesto_remove_root_league_created_faction_membership = yes
			utopia_manifesto_remove_root_league_created_guarantees = yes
		}
		utopia_manifesto_clear_association_charters_for_annexed_partner = yes
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_candidate_targets value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner }
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsor_targets value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner }
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_action_targets value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner }
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_technical_targets value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner }
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_reserve_pending_targets value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner }
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_invited_targets value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner }
		remove_targeted_decision = { target = event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner decision = mission_utopia_league_sponsorship_answer }
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_pending_targets value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner }
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_pending_targets value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner }
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_association_duties_targets value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner }
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_settlement_agreement_targets value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner }
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_long_supply_contract_targets value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner }
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_island_project_lessor_targets value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner }
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_autonomy_partners value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner }
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_members value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner }
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_observers value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner }
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsors value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner }
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_aid_targets value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner }
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_reserve_targets value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner }
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_defense_targets value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner }
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_created_recognized_partners value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner }
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_external_partners value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner }
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_compacts value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner }
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_associates value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner }
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_external_network_members value = event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner }

		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = utopia_manifesto_league_technical_targets^num
					value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			if = { limit = { has_active_mission = mission_utopia_technical_mission } remove_mission = mission_utopia_technical_mission }
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_technical_mission_active
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = utopia_manifesto_league_reserve_pending_targets^num
					value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			if = { limit = { has_active_mission = mission_utopia_reserve_compact_answer } remove_mission = mission_utopia_reserve_compact_answer }
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_reserve_compact_pending
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = utopia_manifesto_league_invited_targets^num
					value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			if = { limit = { has_active_mission = mission_utopia_league_invitation_answer } remove_mission = mission_utopia_league_invitation_answer }
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_invitation_pending
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_pending_targets^num
					value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_pending
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_pending_targets^num
					value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_pending
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_league_technical_targets^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_league_reserve_pending_targets^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_league_invited_targets^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_pending_targets^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals }
			}
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_objective_active
		}

		event_target:utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_partner = {
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_candidate
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsor_candidate
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_technical_mission_host
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_reserve_compact_invited
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_invited
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_member
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_observer
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsor
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_aid_received
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_reserve_contributor
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_defense_partner
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_autonomy_guaranteed
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_obligation_breached
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_request_pending
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_response_technical
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_response_guarantee
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_response_refuse
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_founder_betrayal_pending
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_response_leave
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_response_remain
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_survivor
			remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_pending_founders value = ROOT }
			remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_pending_founders value = ROOT }
			remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_association_founders value = ROOT }
			utopia_manifesto_force_unregister_root_as_league_founder = yes
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = utopia_manifesto_annexed_league_member_recorded
					value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one
					compare = equals
				}
				has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_initialized
				NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_cleanup_in_progress }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_collapsed }
			}
			utopia_manifesto_record_league_failure = yes
		}
		else = { utopia_manifesto_refresh_league_state = yes }
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_dynamic_costs = yes
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_formation_proof = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_clear_league_candidate_targets = {
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_candidate_targets clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_candidate }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsor_targets clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsor_candidate }
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_candidate_targets
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsor_targets
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_action_targets
}

utopia_manifesto_offer_current_scope_as_league_action_target = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = yes
			uses_normal_civilian_systems = yes
			NOT = { tag = ROOT }
			NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
			NOT = { utopia_manifesto_candidate_has_terminal_identity = yes }
			ROOT = {
				NOT = {
					is_in_array = {
						array = utopia_manifesto_league_action_targets
						value = PREV
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ROOT = { add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_action_targets value = PREV } }
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_prepare_league_action_targets = {
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_action_targets
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_candidate_targets utopia_manifesto_offer_current_scope_as_league_action_target = yes }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_external_partners utopia_manifesto_offer_current_scope_as_league_action_target = yes }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_members utopia_manifesto_offer_current_scope_as_league_action_target = yes }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_observers utopia_manifesto_offer_current_scope_as_league_action_target = yes }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_reserve_targets utopia_manifesto_offer_current_scope_as_league_action_target = yes }
}

utopia_manifesto_prepare_league_candidate_targets = {
	utopia_manifesto_clear_league_candidate_targets = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_initialized
			NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_collapsed }
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = { utopia_manifesto_league_candidate_is_valid = yes }
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_candidate
			ROOT = { add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_candidate_targets value = PREV } }
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = { utopia_manifesto_league_sponsor_candidate_is_valid = yes }
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsor_candidate
			ROOT = { add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsor_targets value = PREV } }
		}
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_league_action_targets = yes
	}
}

# Partner scope. ROOT is the exact Event 15 League founder. Each relation is
# revoked only when the partner-side attribution array records that founder as
# its creator. Pre-existing and independently created guarantees survive.
utopia_manifesto_remove_root_league_created_guarantees = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_founder_guarantee_founders value = ROOT } }
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_founder_guarantee_founders value = ROOT }
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_guaranteed_by = ROOT
				NOT = { utopia_manifesto_has_event15_founder_guarantee_creator_for_root = yes }
			}
			ROOT = { diplomatic_relation = { country = PREV relation = guarantee active = no } }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_member_guarantee_founders value = ROOT } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_guaranteed = ROOT }
			diplomatic_relation = { country = ROOT relation = guarantee active = no }
		}
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_member_guarantee_founders value = ROOT }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsor_guarantee_founders value = ROOT } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_guaranteed = ROOT }
			diplomatic_relation = { country = ROOT relation = guarantee active = no }
		}
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsor_guarantee_founders value = ROOT }
	}
}

# Partner scope. ROOT is the exact League founder. Membership is attributed
# only after this partner actually joins the Event 15 faction template.
utopia_manifesto_join_root_league_faction_if_unaligned = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_in_faction = no
			ROOT = {
				has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_formal_defense
				is_faction_leader = yes
				has_faction_template = faction_template_utopia_manifesto_commonwealth_league
			}
		}
		ROOT = { add_to_faction = PREV }
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_faction_with = ROOT
				has_faction_template = faction_template_utopia_manifesto_commonwealth_league
				NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_faction_founders value = ROOT } }
			}
			add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_faction_founders value = ROOT }
		}
	}
}

# Partner scope. Exact creator attribution plus the unique faction template is
# required before leaving; an unrelated or externally replaced faction survives.
utopia_manifesto_remove_root_league_created_faction_membership = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_faction_founders value = ROOT } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_faction = yes
				has_faction_template = faction_template_utopia_manifesto_commonwealth_league
				ROOT = { NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_post_founder_defense_league_survives } }
			}
			leave_faction = yes
		}
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_faction_founders value = ROOT }
	}
}

# Partner scope. ROOT is the founder. The shared League-package source marker
# survives while another exact League recognition role is still live. After
# the final role ends, the marker is removed and visible recognition remains
# only while a non-League basis still supports the same pair.
utopia_manifesto_remove_root_league_created_recognition_if_unbased = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_created_recognized_partners value = PREV }
			}
			NOT = { utopia_manifesto_has_live_league_recognition_role_for_root = yes }
		}
		ROOT = { remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_created_recognized_partners value = PREV } }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { utopia_manifesto_has_independent_league_recognition_basis_for_root = yes } }
			ROOT = { remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_external_partners value = PREV } }
		}
	}
}

# Founder scope. FROM is the exact full-invitation or reserve-compact target.
# Response state and either matching pending pair are closed before an accepted
# package is applied, preventing a delayed event from surviving terminal League
# teardown.
utopia_manifesto_close_from_league_invitation_response = {
	remove_targeted_decision = { target = FROM decision = mission_utopia_league_invitation_answer }
	remove_targeted_decision = { target = FROM decision = mission_utopia_reserve_compact_answer }
	remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_invited_targets value = FROM }
	remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_reserve_pending_targets value = FROM }
	FROM = {
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_invited
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_reserve_compact_invited
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_response_join
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_response_observer
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_response_reserve
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_response_refuse
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_response_guarantee
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = utopia_manifesto_league_invited_targets^num
				value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_invitation_pending
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_invited_targets
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = utopia_manifesto_league_reserve_pending_targets^num
				value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_reserve_compact_pending
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_reserve_pending_targets
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_league_technical_targets^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_league_reserve_pending_targets^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_league_invited_targets^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_pending_targets^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals }
		}
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_objective_active
	}
	utopia_manifesto_reconcile_league_reverse_links = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_resolve_from_league_invitation_response = {
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_from_is_exact_pending_league_invitation_response = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_response_join } }
			utopia_manifesto_close_from_league_invitation_response = yes
			utopia_manifesto_add_from_as_league_member = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_response_observer } }
			utopia_manifesto_close_from_league_invitation_response = yes
			utopia_manifesto_record_from_league_observer = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_response_reserve } }
			utopia_manifesto_close_from_league_invitation_response = yes
			utopia_manifesto_record_from_league_reserve_contribution = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_response_guarantee } }
			utopia_manifesto_close_from_league_invitation_response = yes
			utopia_manifesto_add_from_as_league_member = yes
			utopia_manifesto_record_from_league_defense_consultation = yes
		}
		else = {
			utopia_manifesto_close_from_league_invitation_response = yes
			utopia_manifesto_record_from_league_refusal = yes
		}
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_achievement_league_proofs = yes
	}
	else = { utopia_manifesto_close_from_league_invitation_response = yes }
}

# Founder scope. FROM is the exact sponsor request target. This clears one
# pair without disturbing another founder that may also have a live request.
utopia_manifesto_clear_from_league_sponsorship_request = {
	remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_pending_targets value = FROM }
	FROM = {
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_pending_founders value = ROOT }
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_response_technical
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_response_guarantee
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_response_refuse
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_pending_founders^num
					value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_request_pending
			clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_pending_founders
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_pending_targets^num
				value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_pending
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_objective_active
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_pending_targets
	}
	utopia_manifesto_reconcile_league_reverse_links = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_close_from_league_sponsorship_request = {
	remove_targeted_decision = { target = FROM decision = mission_utopia_league_sponsorship_answer }
	utopia_manifesto_clear_from_league_sponsorship_request = yes
}

# Founder scope. FROM is the exact responding major. A technical sponsorship
# records no guarantee; the defensive package records only a newly created
# sponsor-to-founder guarantee.
utopia_manifesto_apply_from_league_sponsor_guarantee_package = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			FROM = {
				exists = yes
				NOT = { has_guaranteed = ROOT }
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			give_guarantee = ROOT
			if = {
				limit = { has_guaranteed = ROOT }
				add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsor_guarantee_founders value = ROOT }
			}
		}
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_resolve_from_league_sponsorship_response = {
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_from_is_exact_pending_league_sponsorship_response = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_response_technical } }
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_accepted
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_sponsor_technical_aid
			utopia_manifesto_record_from_league_sponsor = yes
			set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_plenty_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_narrative_delta.small }
			utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_ledger_delta = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_response_guarantee } }
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_accepted
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_sponsor_defense_guarantee
			utopia_manifesto_record_from_league_sponsor = yes
			utopia_manifesto_apply_from_league_sponsor_guarantee_package = yes
			set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_cohesion_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_narrative_delta.negative_small }
			utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_league_cohesion_delta = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_sponsor_refused
			set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_cohesion_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_narrative_delta.medium }
			utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_league_cohesion_delta = yes
		}
		utopia_manifesto_close_from_league_sponsorship_request = yes
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_league_state = yes
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_formation_proof = yes
	}
	else = { utopia_manifesto_close_from_league_sponsorship_request = yes }
}

utopia_manifesto_timeout_from_league_sponsorship_request = {
	FROM = {
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_response_technical
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_response_guarantee
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_response_refuse
	}
	utopia_manifesto_resolve_from_league_sponsorship_response = yes
}

# Founder scope. FROM is the exact member attacked by the founder. The pair is
# cleared before exit or failure effects can collapse the wider League.
utopia_manifesto_clear_from_league_betrayal_response = {
	remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_pending_targets value = FROM }
	FROM = {
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_pending_founders value = ROOT }
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_response_leave
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_response_remain
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_pending_founders^num
					value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_founder_betrayal_pending
			clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_pending_founders
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_pending_targets^num
				value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_pending
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_pending_targets
	}
	utopia_manifesto_reconcile_league_reverse_links = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_resolve_from_league_betrayal_response = {
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_from_is_exact_pending_league_betrayal_response = yes }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_founder_betrayal
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_achievement_disqualifier_league_coercion
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_response_leave } }
			utopia_manifesto_clear_from_league_betrayal_response = yes
			utopia_manifesto_record_from_league_exit = yes
		}
		else = {
			FROM = { set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_survivor }
			utopia_manifesto_clear_from_league_betrayal_response = yes
		}
		utopia_manifesto_record_league_failure = yes
	}
	else = { utopia_manifesto_clear_from_league_betrayal_response = yes }
}

utopia_manifesto_clear_league_pending_objectives = {
	if = { limit = { has_active_mission = mission_utopia_technical_mission } remove_mission = mission_utopia_technical_mission }
	if = { limit = { has_active_mission = mission_utopia_reserve_compact_answer } remove_mission = mission_utopia_reserve_compact_answer }
	if = { limit = { has_active_mission = mission_utopia_league_invitation_answer } remove_mission = mission_utopia_league_invitation_answer }
	if = { limit = { has_active_mission = mission_utopia_prove_league_not_mask } remove_mission = mission_utopia_prove_league_not_mask }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_technical_targets clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_technical_mission_host }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_reserve_pending_targets clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_reserve_compact_invited }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_invited_targets clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_invited }
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_pending_targets
		ROOT = { remove_targeted_decision = { target = PREV decision = mission_utopia_league_sponsorship_answer } }
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_pending_founders value = ROOT }
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_response_technical
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_response_guarantee
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_response_refuse
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_pending_founders^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals } }
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_request_pending
			clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_pending_founders
		}
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_pending_targets
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_pending_founders value = ROOT }
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_response_leave
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_response_remain
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_pending_founders^num value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero compare = equals } }
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_founder_betrayal_pending
			clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_pending_founders
		}
	}
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_technical_targets
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_reserve_pending_targets
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_invited_targets
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_pending_targets
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_pending_targets
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_technical_mission_active
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_reserve_compact_pending
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_invitation_pending
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_pending
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_pending
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_legitimacy_mission_active
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_objective_active
}

utopia_manifesto_initialize_league = {
	utopia_manifesto_clear_league_pending_objectives = yes
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_collapsed
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_initialized
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_cohesion = constant:utopia_manifesto_league_limits.cohesion_default }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_member_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_aid_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_reserve_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_defense_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_shared_action_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_refusal_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_exit_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_failure_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_league_state = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_add_from_as_league_recognized_partner = {
	if = {
		limit = { FROM = { exists = yes } }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_external_partners value = FROM } } }
			add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_external_partners value = FROM }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_created_recognized_partners value = FROM } } }
			add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_created_recognized_partners value = FROM }
		}
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_league_cohesion_delta = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_league_cohesion_delta } }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_cohesion_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	}
	add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_cohesion = utopia_manifesto_league_cohesion_delta }
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_league_state = yes
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_cohesion_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
}

utopia_manifesto_refresh_league_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_league_cohesion }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = utopia_manifesto_league_cohesion
			min = constant:utopia_manifesto_league_limits.cohesion_minimum
			max = constant:utopia_manifesto_league_limits.cohesion_maximum
		}
	}
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_member_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_aid_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_reserve_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_defense_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_league_members
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			ROOT = { add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_member_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one } }
		}
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_league_aid_targets
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			ROOT = { add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_aid_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one } }
		}
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_league_reserve_targets
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			ROOT = { add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_reserve_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one } }
		}
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_league_defense_targets
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			ROOT = { add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_defense_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one } }
		}
	}
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_refusal_count = utopia_manifesto_league_refusals^num }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_exit_count = utopia_manifesto_league_exits^num }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_shared_action_count = utopia_manifesto_league_aid_count }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_shared_action_count = utopia_manifesto_league_reserve_count }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_shared_action_count = utopia_manifesto_league_defense_count }
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_compacts
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = yes
				NOT = {
					is_in_array = {
						array = ROOT.utopia_manifesto_recognized_external_partners
						value = THIS
					}
				}
			}
			ROOT = { add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_external_partners value = PREV } }
		}
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_associates
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = yes
				NOT = {
					is_in_array = {
						array = ROOT.utopia_manifesto_recognized_external_partners
						value = THIS
					}
				}
			}
			ROOT = { add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_external_partners value = PREV } }
		}
	}
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_external_network_members
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_league_members
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			ROOT = {
				add_to_array = {
					array = utopia_manifesto_external_network_members
					value = PREV
				}
			}
		}
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_external_partners
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = yes
				NOT = {
					is_in_array = {
						array = ROOT.utopia_manifesto_external_network_members
						value = THIS
					}
				}
			}
			ROOT = {
				add_to_array = {
					array = utopia_manifesto_external_network_members
					value = PREV
				}
			}
		}
	}
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_external_network_count = utopia_manifesto_external_network_members^num }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = utopia_manifesto_external_network_count
				value = constant:utopia_manifesto_league_limits.minimum_formation_network
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_first_associate_recognized
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = utopia_manifesto_external_network_count
				value = constant:utopia_manifesto_league_limits.small_places_compact_network
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_small_places_compact
	}
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_has_regional_proclamation_network = yes }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_regional_commonwealth
	}
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_stable
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_strong
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_initialized
			utopia_manifesto_league_is_stable = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_stable
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_initialized
			utopia_manifesto_league_is_strong = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_strong
	}
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_event_reachability = yes
	utopia_manifesto_prepare_league_candidate_targets = yes
	utopia_manifesto_reconcile_league_reverse_links = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_invite_from_to_league = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			utopia_manifesto_league_entry_requirements_met = yes
			FROM = { utopia_manifesto_league_candidate_is_valid = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_invited } } }
			add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_invited_targets value = FROM }
			FROM = { set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_invited }
			utopia_manifesto_reconcile_league_reverse_links = yes
		}
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_add_from_as_league_member = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			FROM = {
				exists = yes
				NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_member }
				NOT = { utopia_manifesto_has_conflicting_league_partner_relationship_for_root = yes }
			}
		}
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_refusals value = FROM }
		add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_members value = FROM }
		utopia_manifesto_add_from_as_league_recognized_partner = yes
		FROM = {
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_refused
