			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_member
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_autonomy_guaranteed
		}
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_cohesion_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_league_change.entry }
		utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_league_cohesion_delta = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_formal_defense
				FROM = { is_in_faction = no }
			}
			FROM = { utopia_manifesto_join_root_league_faction_if_unaligned = yes }
		}
		utopia_manifesto_try_formalize_league_defense = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_remove_from_league = {
	if = {
		limit = { FROM = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_member } }
		utopia_manifesto_clear_from_league_betrayal_response = yes
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_members value = FROM }
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_defense_targets value = FROM }
		FROM = {
			utopia_manifesto_remove_root_league_created_faction_membership = yes
			utopia_manifesto_remove_root_league_created_guarantees = yes
			utopia_manifesto_remove_root_league_created_recognition_if_unbased = yes
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_member
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_defense_partner
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_autonomy_guaranteed
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_obligation_breached
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_founder_betrayal_pending
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_response_leave
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_response_remain
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_survivor
		}
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_league_state = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_record_from_league_observer = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			FROM = {
				exists = yes
				uses_normal_civilian_systems = yes
				NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_observer }
				NOT = { utopia_manifesto_has_conflicting_league_partner_relationship_for_root = yes }
			}
		}
		add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_observers value = FROM }
		utopia_manifesto_add_from_as_league_recognized_partner = yes
		FROM = {
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_refused
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_observer
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_autonomy_guaranteed
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_has_observer
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_cohesion_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_league_change.entry }
		utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_league_cohesion_delta = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_record_from_league_refusal = {
	if = {
		limit = { FROM = { exists = yes } }
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_refused } } }
			add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_refusals value = FROM }
			add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_refusal_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one }
		}
		FROM = {
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_refused
		}
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_cohesion_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_league_change.refusal }
		utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_league_cohesion_delta = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_record_from_league_aid = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			FROM = {
				exists = yes
				NOT = { utopia_manifesto_has_conflicting_league_partner_relationship_for_root = yes }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_aid_received } } }
			add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_aid_targets value = FROM }
			utopia_manifesto_add_from_as_league_recognized_partner = yes
			FROM = { set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_aid_received }
			set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_cohesion_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_league_change.aid }
			utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_league_cohesion_delta = yes
		}
		utopia_manifesto_try_formalize_league_defense = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_record_from_league_reserve_contribution = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			FROM = {
				exists = yes
				uses_normal_civilian_systems = yes
				NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
				NOT = { utopia_manifesto_has_conflicting_league_partner_relationship_for_root = yes }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_reserve_contributor } } }
			add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_reserve_targets value = FROM }
			utopia_manifesto_add_from_as_league_recognized_partner = yes
			FROM = {
				clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_refused
				set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_reserve_contributor
				set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_autonomy_guaranteed
			}
			set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_cohesion_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_league_change.reserve }
			utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_league_cohesion_delta = yes
		}
		utopia_manifesto_try_formalize_league_defense = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_record_from_league_defense_consultation = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_members value = FROM }
			FROM = {
				has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_member
				NOT = { utopia_manifesto_has_conflicting_league_partner_relationship_for_root = yes }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_defense_partner } } }
			add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_defense_targets value = FROM }
			FROM = { set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_defense_partner }
			if = {
				limit = { FROM = { NOT = { is_guaranteed_by = ROOT } } }
				ROOT = { give_guarantee = FROM }
				if = {
					limit = {
						FROM = {
							is_guaranteed_by = ROOT
							NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_founder_guarantee_founders value = ROOT } }
						}
					}
					FROM = { add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_founder_guarantee_founders value = ROOT } }
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					FROM = {
						utopia_manifesto_has_event15_founder_guarantee_creator_for_root = yes
						NOT = { is_in_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_founder_guarantee_founders value = ROOT } }
					}
				}
				FROM = { add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_founder_guarantee_founders value = ROOT } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { FROM = { NOT = { has_guaranteed = ROOT } } }
				FROM = {
					give_guarantee = ROOT
					if = {
						limit = { has_guaranteed = ROOT }
						add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_member_guarantee_founders value = ROOT }
					}
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_cohesion_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_league_change.defense }
			utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_league_cohesion_delta = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_defense_proof_complete
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_formal_defense_vote_passed
		utopia_manifesto_try_formalize_league_defense = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_record_from_league_sponsor = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			FROM = {
				exists = yes
				NOT = { utopia_manifesto_has_conflicting_league_partner_relationship_for_root = yes }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsor } } }
			add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsors value = FROM }
			if = {
				limit = { FROM = { NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_observer } } }
				add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_observers value = FROM }
			}
			utopia_manifesto_add_from_as_league_recognized_partner = yes
			FROM = {
				set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsor
				set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_observer
				set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_autonomy_guaranteed
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = utopia_manifesto_opinion_partnership }
			}
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_has_observer
			set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_cohesion_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_league_change.sponsor }
			utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_league_cohesion_delta = yes
		}
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_try_formalize_league_defense = {
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_league_state = yes
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_league_can_formalize_defense = yes }
		create_faction_from_template = {
			template = faction_template_utopia_manifesto_commonwealth_league
			name = utopia_manifesto_commonwealth_league
			icon = GFX_faction_logo_generic_democratic
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_formal_defense
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_autonomy_charter_active
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_governance_household_council
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_governance_common_table
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_governance_guardians
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_governance_island_secretariat
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_governance_jest_council
		if = { limit = { utopia_manifesto_is_consent_route = yes } set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_governance_household_council }
		else_if = { limit = { utopia_manifesto_is_common_table_route = yes } set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_governance_common_table }
		else_if = { limit = { utopia_manifesto_is_guardians_of_measure_route = yes } set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_governance_guardians }
		else_if = { limit = { utopia_manifesto_is_closed_island_route = yes } set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_governance_island_secretariat }
		else = { set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_governance_jest_council }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_league_members
			utopia_manifesto_join_root_league_faction_if_unaligned = yes
		}
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_record_from_league_exit = {
	if = {
		limit = { FROM = { exists = yes } }
		utopia_manifesto_remove_from_league = yes
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_exited } } }
			add_to_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_exits value = FROM }
			FROM = { set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_exited }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_cohesion_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_league_change.exit }
		utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_league_cohesion_delta = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_formal_defense
				check_variable = {
					var = utopia_manifesto_league_member_count
					value = constant:utopia_manifesto_league_limits.formal_defense_members
					compare = less_than
				}
			}
			utopia_manifesto_record_league_failure = yes
		}
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_record_league_failure = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_cleanup_in_progress }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_collapsed }
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_failure
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_failure_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_league_cohesion_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_league_change.failure }
		utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_league_cohesion_delta = yes
		if = {
			limit = { utopia_manifesto_league_failure_limit_reached = yes }
			utopia_manifesto_collapse_league = yes
		}
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_collapse_league = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_collapsed } }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_collapsed
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_failure
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_cleanup_in_progress
		utopia_manifesto_clear_league_pending_objectives = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_formal_defense
				is_faction_leader = yes
				has_faction_template = faction_template_utopia_manifesto_commonwealth_league
			}
			dismantle_faction = yes
		}
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_league_members
			utopia_manifesto_remove_root_league_created_faction_membership = yes
			utopia_manifesto_remove_root_league_created_guarantees = yes
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_member
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_autonomy_guaranteed
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_defense_partner
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_obligation_breached
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_founder_betrayal_pending
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_response_leave
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_response_remain
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_survivor
		}
		for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_reverse_link_targets utopia_manifesto_remove_root_league_created_faction_membership = yes utopia_manifesto_remove_root_league_created_guarantees = yes }
		for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_defense_targets utopia_manifesto_remove_root_league_created_guarantees = yes }
		for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_observers clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_observer clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_autonomy_guaranteed }
		for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsors utopia_manifesto_remove_root_league_created_guarantees = yes clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsor }
		for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_reserve_targets clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_reserve_contributor }
		for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_aid_targets clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_aid_received }
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_members
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_observers
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsors
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_reserve_targets
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_aid_targets
		clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_defense_targets
		clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_league_created_recognition_cleanup_snapshot
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_league_created_recognized_partners
			add_to_temp_array = { utopia_manifesto_league_created_recognition_cleanup_snapshot = THIS }
		}
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = utopia_manifesto_league_created_recognition_cleanup_snapshot
			utopia_manifesto_remove_root_league_created_recognition_if_unbased = yes
		}
		clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_league_created_recognition_cleanup_snapshot
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_initialized
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_formal_defense
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_autonomy_charter_active
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_has_observer
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_accepted
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_founder_betrayal
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_stable
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_strong
		clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_cleanup_in_progress
		utopia_manifesto_refresh_league_state = yes
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_clear_league_runtime = {
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_cleanup_in_progress
	utopia_manifesto_clear_league_pending_objectives = yes
	utopia_manifesto_clear_league_candidate_targets = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_formal_defense
			is_faction_leader = yes
			has_faction_template = faction_template_utopia_manifesto_commonwealth_league
		}
		dismantle_faction = yes
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_members utopia_manifesto_remove_root_league_created_faction_membership = yes utopia_manifesto_remove_root_league_created_guarantees = yes clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_member clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_obligation_breached clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_founder_betrayal_pending clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_response_leave clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_response_remain clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_betrayal_survivor }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_reverse_link_targets utopia_manifesto_remove_root_league_created_faction_membership = yes utopia_manifesto_remove_root_league_created_guarantees = yes }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_refusals clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_refused }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsors utopia_manifesto_remove_root_league_created_guarantees = yes clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsor }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_aid_targets clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_aid_received }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_reserve_targets clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_reserve_contributor }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_defense_targets utopia_manifesto_remove_root_league_created_guarantees = yes clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_defense_partner }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_exits clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_exited }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_observers clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_observer clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_autonomy_guaranteed }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_league_members clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_autonomy_guaranteed }
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_members
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_refusals
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsors
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_aid_targets
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_reserve_targets
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_defense_targets
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_exits
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_observers
	clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_league_created_recognition_cleanup_snapshot
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_league_created_recognized_partners
		add_to_temp_array = { utopia_manifesto_league_created_recognition_cleanup_snapshot = THIS }
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_league_created_recognition_cleanup_snapshot
		utopia_manifesto_remove_root_league_created_recognition_if_unbased = yes
	}
	clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_league_created_recognition_cleanup_snapshot
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_league_created_recognized_partners
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_initialized
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_collapsed
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_formal_defense
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_formal_defense_vote_passed
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_autonomy_charter_active
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_has_observer
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_sponsorship_accepted
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_founder_betrayal
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_sponsor_technical_aid
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_sponsor_defense_guarantee
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_sponsor_refused
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_governance_household_council
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_governance_common_table
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_governance_guardians
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_governance_island_secretariat
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_governance_jest_council
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_stable
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_strong
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_failure
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_broad_recognition_proven
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_league_cleanup_in_progress
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_league_cohesion
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_league_member_count
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_league_aid_count
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_league_reserve_count
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_league_defense_count
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_league_shared_action_count
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_league_refusal_count
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_league_exit_count
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_league_failure_count
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_external_network_count
	utopia_manifesto_reconcile_league_reverse_links = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_clear_external_network_runtime = {
	utopia_manifesto_clear_all_association_charter_links = yes
	clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_colony_association_cleanup_snapshot
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_colony_association_partners
		add_to_temp_array = { utopia_manifesto_colony_association_cleanup_snapshot = THIS }
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_colony_association_cleanup_snapshot utopia_manifesto_remove_root_colony_association = yes }
	clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_colony_association_cleanup_snapshot
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_colony_association_partners
	clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_autonomy_partner_cleanup_snapshot
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_autonomy_partners
		add_to_temp_array = { utopia_manifesto_autonomy_partner_cleanup_snapshot = THIS }
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_autonomy_partner_cleanup_snapshot utopia_manifesto_remove_root_stewardship_autonomy_pact = yes }
	clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_autonomy_partner_cleanup_snapshot
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_stewardship_autonomy_partners
	clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_compact_partner_cleanup_snapshot
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_compacts
		add_to_temp_array = { utopia_manifesto_compact_partner_cleanup_snapshot = THIS }
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = utopia_manifesto_compact_partner_cleanup_snapshot utopia_manifesto_remove_root_non_autonomy_compact_sources = yes }
	clear_temp_array = utopia_manifesto_compact_partner_cleanup_snapshot
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_associates
		utopia_manifesto_remove_root_association_diplomacy = yes
		remove_from_array = { array = utopia_manifesto_association_founders value = ROOT }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = utopia_manifesto_association_founders^num
					value = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_associate_partner
			clear_array = utopia_manifesto_association_founders
			utopia_manifesto_clear_association_review_runtime = yes
		}
	}
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_compacts
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_associates
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_recognized_external_partners
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_external_network_members
	clear_array = utopia_manifesto_foreign_reaction_contacts
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_external_network_count
	utopia_manifesto_reconcile_league_reverse_links = yes
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Formation proof and regional proclamation threshold
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

utopia_manifesto_refresh_formation_proof = {
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_formation_proof_met
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_formation_proof_route
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_voluntary_formation_proof_met = yes }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_formation_proof_met
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_formation_proof_route = constant:utopia_manifesto_route.consent_of_households }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_council_formation_proof_met = yes }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_formation_proof_met
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_formation_proof_route = constant:utopia_manifesto_route.common_table }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_planned_formation_proof_met = yes }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_formation_proof_met
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_formation_proof_route = constant:utopia_manifesto_route.guardians_of_measure }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_closed_formation_proof_met = yes }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_formation_proof_met
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_formation_proof_route = constant:utopia_manifesto_route.closed_island }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_practical_formation_proof_met = yes }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_formation_proof_met
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_formation_proof_route = constant:utopia_manifesto_route.joke_understood }
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_complete_formation = {
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_formation_proof = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_formation_proof_met }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_commonwealth_formed
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_commonwealth_of_places
		utopia_manifesto_evaluate_active_evolutions = yes
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Evolution prefire, active evaluation, scheduling, application, and logging
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

utopia_manifesto_prepare_evolution_context = {
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_event_id = constant:utopia_manifesto_event.id }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_type = constant:utopia_manifesto_event.evolution_type }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_stage = utopia_manifesto_evolution_context_id }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_tier = utopia_manifesto_evolution_context_id }
}

utopia_manifesto_evaluate_prefire_evolutions = {
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_prefire_glosses_enabled
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_prefire_necessary_shores_enabled
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_prefire_cities_enabled
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_prefire_nowhere_law_enabled
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_prefire_perfect_island_enabled

	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_evolution_context_id = constant:utopia_manifesto_evolution.glosses_in_the_margin }
	utopia_manifesto_prepare_evolution_context = yes
	if = {
		limit = { is_current_evolution_enabled = yes }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_prefire_glosses_enabled
	}
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_evolution_context_id = constant:utopia_manifesto_evolution.necessary_shores }
	utopia_manifesto_prepare_evolution_context = yes
	if = {
		limit = { is_current_evolution_enabled = yes }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_prefire_necessary_shores_enabled
	}
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_evolution_context_id = constant:utopia_manifesto_evolution.cities_of_one_measure }
	utopia_manifesto_prepare_evolution_context = yes
	if = {
		limit = { is_current_evolution_enabled = yes }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_prefire_cities_enabled
	}
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_evolution_context_id = constant:utopia_manifesto_evolution.nowhere_made_law }
	utopia_manifesto_prepare_evolution_context = yes
	if = {
		limit = { is_current_evolution_enabled = yes }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_prefire_nowhere_law_enabled
	}
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_evolution_context_id = constant:utopia_manifesto_evolution.perfect_island }
	utopia_manifesto_prepare_evolution_context = yes
	if = {
		limit = { is_current_evolution_enabled = yes }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_prefire_perfect_island_enabled
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_consume_prefire_evolution_state = {
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_glosses_setting_enabled
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_necessary_shores_setting_enabled
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_cities_setting_enabled
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_nowhere_law_setting_enabled
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_perfect_island_setting_enabled

	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_evolution_context_id = constant:utopia_manifesto_evolution.glosses_in_the_margin }
	utopia_manifesto_prepare_evolution_context = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_prefire_glosses_enabled
			is_current_evolution_enabled = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_glosses_setting_enabled
	}
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_evolution_context_id = constant:utopia_manifesto_evolution.necessary_shores }
	utopia_manifesto_prepare_evolution_context = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_prefire_necessary_shores_enabled
			is_current_evolution_enabled = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_necessary_shores_setting_enabled
	}
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_evolution_context_id = constant:utopia_manifesto_evolution.cities_of_one_measure }
	utopia_manifesto_prepare_evolution_context = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_prefire_cities_enabled
			is_current_evolution_enabled = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_cities_setting_enabled
	}
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_evolution_context_id = constant:utopia_manifesto_evolution.nowhere_made_law }
	utopia_manifesto_prepare_evolution_context = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_prefire_nowhere_law_enabled
			is_current_evolution_enabled = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_nowhere_law_setting_enabled
	}
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_evolution_context_id = constant:utopia_manifesto_evolution.perfect_island }
	utopia_manifesto_prepare_evolution_context = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_prefire_perfect_island_enabled
			is_current_evolution_enabled = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_perfect_island_setting_enabled
	}
	utopia_manifesto_consume_prepared_prefire_evolution_choice = yes
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_prefire_evolution_state_consumed
}

utopia_manifesto_clear_evolution_availability = {
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_glosses_available
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_necessary_shores_available
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_cities_available
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_nowhere_law_available
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_perfect_island_available
}

utopia_manifesto_evaluate_active_evolutions = {
	utopia_manifesto_validate_scheduled_evolution = yes
	utopia_manifesto_clear_evolution_availability = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_glosses_setting_enabled
			utopia_manifesto_glosses_in_the_margin_requirements_met = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_evolution_context_id = constant:utopia_manifesto_evolution.glosses_in_the_margin }
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_evolution_context = yes
		if = { limit = { is_current_evolution_enabled = yes } set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_glosses_available }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_necessary_shores_setting_enabled
			utopia_manifesto_necessary_shores_requirements_met = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_evolution_context_id = constant:utopia_manifesto_evolution.necessary_shores }
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_evolution_context = yes
		if = { limit = { is_current_evolution_enabled = yes } set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_necessary_shores_available }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_cities_setting_enabled
			utopia_manifesto_cities_of_one_measure_requirements_met = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_evolution_context_id = constant:utopia_manifesto_evolution.cities_of_one_measure }
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_evolution_context = yes
		if = { limit = { is_current_evolution_enabled = yes } set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_cities_available }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_nowhere_law_setting_enabled
			utopia_manifesto_nowhere_made_law_requirements_met = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_evolution_context_id = constant:utopia_manifesto_evolution.nowhere_made_law }
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_evolution_context = yes
		if = { limit = { is_current_evolution_enabled = yes } set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_nowhere_law_available }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_perfect_island_setting_enabled
			utopia_manifesto_perfect_island_requirements_met = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_evolution_context_id = constant:utopia_manifesto_evolution.perfect_island }
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_evolution_context = yes
		if = { limit = { is_current_evolution_enabled = yes } set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_perfect_island_available }
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_schedule_prepared_evolution = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_evolution_to_schedule }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_evolution_context_id = utopia_manifesto_evolution_to_schedule }
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_evolution_context = yes
		if = {
			limit = { is_current_evolution_enabled = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_evolution_delay_days } }
				set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_evolution_delay_days = constant:utopia_manifesto_evolution_pacing.base_days }
			}
			set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_scheduled_evolution = utopia_manifesto_evolution_to_schedule }
			set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_scheduled_evolution_delay_days = utopia_manifesto_evolution_delay_days }
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_pending
		}
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_validate_scheduled_evolution = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_pending
			has_variable = utopia_manifesto_scheduled_evolution
		}
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_evolution_context_id = utopia_manifesto_scheduled_evolution }
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_evolution_context = yes
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_current_evolution_enabled = yes } }
			set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_disabled_before_delivery
			utopia_manifesto_clear_scheduled_evolution = yes
		}
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_clear_scheduled_evolution = {
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_pending
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_scheduled_evolution
	clear_variable = utopia_manifesto_scheduled_evolution_delay_days
}

utopia_manifesto_log_prepared_evolution = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_evolution_to_record }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_evolution_context_id = utopia_manifesto_evolution_to_record }
		utopia_manifesto_prepare_evolution_context = yes
		if = {
			limit = { is_current_evolution_enabled = yes }
			save_event_target_as = events_log_evolution_actor
			set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_has_actor = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one }
			record_events_log_evolution_entry = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_evolution_to_record = constant:utopia_manifesto_evolution.glosses_in_the_margin } }
				set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_glosses_recorded
				set_global_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_glosses_chronology_recorded
				clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_glosses_available
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_evolution_to_record = constant:utopia_manifesto_evolution.necessary_shores } }
				set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_necessary_shores_recorded
				set_global_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_necessary_shores_chronology_recorded
				clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_necessary_shores_available
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_evolution_to_record = constant:utopia_manifesto_evolution.cities_of_one_measure } }
				set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_cities_recorded
				set_global_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_cities_chronology_recorded
				clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_cities_available
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_evolution_to_record = constant:utopia_manifesto_evolution.nowhere_made_law } }
				set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_nowhere_law_recorded
				set_global_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_nowhere_law_chronology_recorded
				clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_nowhere_law_available
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_evolution_to_record = constant:utopia_manifesto_evolution.perfect_island } }
				set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_perfect_island_recorded
				set_global_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_perfect_island_chronology_recorded
				clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_perfect_island_available
			}
			utopia_manifesto_clear_scheduled_evolution = yes
		}
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_apply_glosses_in_the_margin_evolution = {
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_evolution_to_record = constant:utopia_manifesto_evolution.glosses_in_the_margin }
	utopia_manifesto_log_prepared_evolution = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_apply_necessary_shores_evolution = {
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_evolution_to_record = constant:utopia_manifesto_evolution.necessary_shores }
	utopia_manifesto_log_prepared_evolution = yes
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_event_reachability = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_apply_cities_of_one_measure_evolution = {
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_evolution_to_record = constant:utopia_manifesto_evolution.cities_of_one_measure }
	utopia_manifesto_log_prepared_evolution = yes
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_event_reachability = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_apply_nowhere_made_law_evolution = {
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_evolution_to_record = constant:utopia_manifesto_evolution.nowhere_made_law }
	utopia_manifesto_log_prepared_evolution = yes
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_event_reachability = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_apply_perfect_island_evolution = {
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_evolution_to_record = constant:utopia_manifesto_evolution.perfect_island }
	utopia_manifesto_log_prepared_evolution = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_clear_evolution_runtime = {
	utopia_manifesto_clear_prefire_evolution_opening_runtime = yes
	utopia_manifesto_clear_evolution_consumption_runtime = yes
	utopia_manifesto_clear_evolution_availability = yes
	utopia_manifesto_clear_scheduled_evolution = yes
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_prefire_glosses_enabled
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_prefire_necessary_shores_enabled
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_prefire_cities_enabled
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_prefire_nowhere_law_enabled
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_prefire_perfect_island_enabled
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_prefire_evolution_state_consumed
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_glosses_setting_enabled
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_necessary_shores_setting_enabled
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_cities_setting_enabled
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_nowhere_law_setting_enabled
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_perfect_island_setting_enabled
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_evolution_disabled_before_delivery
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Dynamic costs and paid military/institutional growth
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

utopia_manifesto_refresh_military_growth_capacity = {
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_growth_capacity = constant:utopia_manifesto_growth_limits.military_capacity_base }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_capacity_state_scale = num_of_controlled_states }
	multiply_temp_variable = {
		utopia_manifesto_capacity_state_scale = constant:utopia_manifesto_growth_limits.military_capacity_per_controlled_state
	}
	add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_growth_capacity = utopia_manifesto_capacity_state_scale }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_chartered_districts }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_capacity_district_scale = utopia_manifesto_chartered_districts }
		multiply_temp_variable = {
			utopia_manifesto_capacity_district_scale = constant:utopia_manifesto_growth_limits.military_capacity_per_chartered_district
		}
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_growth_capacity = utopia_manifesto_capacity_district_scale }
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = utopia_manifesto_military_growth_capacity
		min = constant:utopia_manifesto_growth_limits.military_capacity_base
		max = constant:utopia_manifesto_growth_limits.military_capacity_maximum
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_military_growth_batches } }
		set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_growth_batches = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_refresh_dynamic_costs = {
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_military_growth_capacity = yes
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_growth_tier_input } }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_growth_tier_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_growth_limits.minimum_tier }
	}
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = utopia_manifesto_growth_tier_input
		min = constant:utopia_manifesto_growth_limits.minimum_tier
		max = constant:utopia_manifesto_growth_limits.maximum_tier
	}
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_growth_tier = utopia_manifesto_growth_tier_input }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_growth_state_scale = num_of_controlled_states }

	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_manpower_cost = constant:utopia_manifesto_dynamic_cost_base.military_manpower }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_infantry_equipment_cost = constant:utopia_manifesto_dynamic_cost_base.military_infantry_equipment }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_support_equipment_cost = constant:utopia_manifesto_dynamic_cost_base.military_support_equipment }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_army_experience_cost = constant:utopia_manifesto_dynamic_cost_base.military_army_experience }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_institutional_manpower_cost = constant:utopia_manifesto_dynamic_cost_base.institutional_manpower }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_institutional_support_equipment_cost = constant:utopia_manifesto_dynamic_cost_base.institutional_support_equipment }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_institutional_political_power_cost = constant:utopia_manifesto_dynamic_cost_base.institutional_political_power }

	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_scaled_cost = constant:utopia_manifesto_dynamic_cost_per_state.military_manpower }
	multiply_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_scaled_cost = utopia_manifesto_growth_state_scale }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_manpower_cost = utopia_manifesto_scaled_cost }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_scaled_cost = constant:utopia_manifesto_dynamic_cost_per_state.military_infantry_equipment }
	multiply_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_scaled_cost = utopia_manifesto_growth_state_scale }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_infantry_equipment_cost = utopia_manifesto_scaled_cost }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_scaled_cost = constant:utopia_manifesto_dynamic_cost_per_state.military_support_equipment }
	multiply_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_scaled_cost = utopia_manifesto_growth_state_scale }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_support_equipment_cost = utopia_manifesto_scaled_cost }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_scaled_cost = constant:utopia_manifesto_dynamic_cost_per_state.military_army_experience }
	multiply_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_scaled_cost = utopia_manifesto_growth_state_scale }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_army_experience_cost = utopia_manifesto_scaled_cost }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_scaled_cost = constant:utopia_manifesto_dynamic_cost_per_state.institutional_manpower }
	multiply_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_scaled_cost = utopia_manifesto_growth_state_scale }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_institutional_manpower_cost = utopia_manifesto_scaled_cost }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_scaled_cost = constant:utopia_manifesto_dynamic_cost_per_state.institutional_support_equipment }
	multiply_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_scaled_cost = utopia_manifesto_growth_state_scale }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_institutional_support_equipment_cost = utopia_manifesto_scaled_cost }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_scaled_cost = constant:utopia_manifesto_dynamic_cost_per_state.institutional_political_power }
	multiply_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_scaled_cost = utopia_manifesto_growth_state_scale }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_institutional_political_power_cost = utopia_manifesto_scaled_cost }

	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_manpower_cost_foundation = utopia_manifesto_military_manpower_cost }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_infantry_equipment_cost_foundation = utopia_manifesto_military_infantry_equipment_cost }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_support_equipment_cost_foundation = utopia_manifesto_military_support_equipment_cost }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_army_experience_cost_foundation = utopia_manifesto_military_army_experience_cost }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_auxiliary_total_support_equipment_cost = utopia_manifesto_military_support_equipment_cost_foundation }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_auxiliary_total_support_equipment_cost = constant:utopia_manifesto_decision_cost.support_medium }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_institutional_manpower_cost_foundation = utopia_manifesto_institutional_manpower_cost }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_institutional_support_equipment_cost_foundation = utopia_manifesto_institutional_support_equipment_cost }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_institutional_political_power_cost_foundation = utopia_manifesto_institutional_political_power_cost }

	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_manpower_cost_network = utopia_manifesto_military_manpower_cost }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_infantry_equipment_cost_network = utopia_manifesto_military_infantry_equipment_cost }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_support_equipment_cost_network = utopia_manifesto_military_support_equipment_cost }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_army_experience_cost_network = utopia_manifesto_military_army_experience_cost }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_institutional_manpower_cost_network = utopia_manifesto_institutional_manpower_cost }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_institutional_support_equipment_cost_network = utopia_manifesto_institutional_support_equipment_cost }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_institutional_political_power_cost_network = utopia_manifesto_institutional_political_power_cost }
	multiply_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_manpower_cost_network = constant:utopia_manifesto_growth_tier.network }
	multiply_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_infantry_equipment_cost_network = constant:utopia_manifesto_growth_tier.network }
	multiply_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_support_equipment_cost_network = constant:utopia_manifesto_growth_tier.network }
	multiply_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_army_experience_cost_network = constant:utopia_manifesto_growth_tier.network }
	multiply_variable = { utopia_manifesto_institutional_manpower_cost_network = constant:utopia_manifesto_growth_tier.network }
	multiply_variable = { utopia_manifesto_institutional_support_equipment_cost_network = constant:utopia_manifesto_growth_tier.network }
	multiply_variable = { utopia_manifesto_institutional_political_power_cost_network = constant:utopia_manifesto_growth_tier.network }

	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_manpower_cost_capstone = utopia_manifesto_military_manpower_cost }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_infantry_equipment_cost_capstone = utopia_manifesto_military_infantry_equipment_cost }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_support_equipment_cost_capstone = utopia_manifesto_military_support_equipment_cost }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_army_experience_cost_capstone = utopia_manifesto_military_army_experience_cost }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_institutional_manpower_cost_capstone = utopia_manifesto_institutional_manpower_cost }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_institutional_support_equipment_cost_capstone = utopia_manifesto_institutional_support_equipment_cost }
	set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_institutional_political_power_cost_capstone = utopia_manifesto_institutional_political_power_cost }
	multiply_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_manpower_cost_capstone = constant:utopia_manifesto_growth_tier.capstone }
	multiply_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_infantry_equipment_cost_capstone = constant:utopia_manifesto_growth_tier.capstone }
	multiply_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_support_equipment_cost_capstone = constant:utopia_manifesto_growth_tier.capstone }
	multiply_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_army_experience_cost_capstone = constant:utopia_manifesto_growth_tier.capstone }
	multiply_variable = { utopia_manifesto_institutional_manpower_cost_capstone = constant:utopia_manifesto_growth_tier.capstone }
	multiply_variable = { utopia_manifesto_institutional_support_equipment_cost_capstone = constant:utopia_manifesto_growth_tier.capstone }
	multiply_variable = { utopia_manifesto_institutional_political_power_cost_capstone = constant:utopia_manifesto_growth_tier.capstone }

	multiply_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_manpower_cost = utopia_manifesto_growth_tier_input }
	multiply_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_infantry_equipment_cost = utopia_manifesto_growth_tier_input }
	multiply_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_support_equipment_cost = utopia_manifesto_growth_tier_input }
	multiply_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_army_experience_cost = utopia_manifesto_growth_tier_input }
	multiply_variable = { utopia_manifesto_institutional_manpower_cost = utopia_manifesto_growth_tier_input }
	multiply_variable = { utopia_manifesto_institutional_support_equipment_cost = utopia_manifesto_growth_tier_input }
	multiply_variable = { utopia_manifesto_institutional_political_power_cost = utopia_manifesto_growth_tier_input }
}

utopia_manifesto_prepare_military_formation_input = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_military_formation_input } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_route_common_table }
			set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_formation_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_military_formation.worker_defense }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_route_closed_island }
			set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_formation_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_military_formation.service_formation }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = utopia_manifesto_growth_tier_input
					value = constant:utopia_manifesto_growth_tier.capstone
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_formation_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_military_formation.commonwealth_guard }
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_formation_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_military_formation.professional_guard }
		}
	}
}

utopia_manifesto_create_paid_formation_template = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_formation_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_military_formation.citizen_watch }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_citizen_watch_template_created }
		}
		meta_effect = {
			text = {
				division_template = {
					name = "[FORMATION_NAME]"
					regiments = {
						infantry = { x = 0 y = 0 }
						infantry = { x = 0 y = 1 }
						infantry = { x = 1 y = 0 }
						infantry = { x = 1 y = 1 }
					}
					support = { engineer = { x = 0 y = 0 } }
				}
			}
			FORMATION_NAME = "[This.GetUtopiaManifestoMilitaryFormationName]"
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_citizen_watch_template_created
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_formation_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_military_formation.worker_defense }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_worker_defense_template_created }
		}
		meta_effect = {
			text = {
				division_template = {
					name = "[FORMATION_NAME]"
					regiments = {
						infantry = { x = 0 y = 0 }
						infantry = { x = 0 y = 1 }
						infantry = { x = 1 y = 0 }
						infantry = { x = 1 y = 1 }
						infantry = { x = 2 y = 0 }
						infantry = { x = 2 y = 1 }
					}
					support = { engineer = { x = 0 y = 0 } }
				}
			}
			FORMATION_NAME = "[This.GetUtopiaManifestoMilitaryFormationName]"
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_worker_defense_template_created
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_formation_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_military_formation.engineer_corps }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_engineer_corps_template_created }
		}
		meta_effect = {
			text = {
				division_template = {
					name = "[FORMATION_NAME]"
					regiments = {
						infantry = { x = 0 y = 0 }
						infantry = { x = 0 y = 1 }
						infantry = { x = 1 y = 0 }
					}
					support = { engineer = { x = 0 y = 0 } }
				}
			}
			FORMATION_NAME = "[This.GetUtopiaManifestoMilitaryFormationName]"
		}
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_engineer_corps_template_created
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_formation_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_military_formation.service_formation }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_service_formation_template_created }
		}
		meta_effect = {
			text = {
				division_template = {
					name = "[FORMATION_NAME]"
					regiments = {
						infantry = { x = 0 y = 0 }
						infantry = { x = 0 y = 1 }
						infantry = { x = 1 y = 0 }
						infantry = { x = 1 y = 1 }
						infantry = { x = 2 y = 0 }
						infantry = { x = 2 y = 1 }
					}
					support = { engineer = { x = 0 y = 0 } }
				}
			}
			FORMATION_NAME = "[This.GetUtopiaManifestoMilitaryFormationName]"
		}

	}

utopia_manifesto_deploy_paid_formation = {
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_paid_growth_unit_count = constant:utopia_manifesto_growth_limits.military_units_per_batch }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_paid_growth_experience = constant:utopia_manifesto_unit_quality.household_guard_start_experience_factor }
	random_controlled_state = {
		limit = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_formation_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_military_formation.citizen_watch } }
			meta_effect = {
				text = { create_unit = { division = "name = \"[FORMATION_NAME]\" division_template = \"[FORMATION_NAME]\" start_experience_factor = [START_EXPERIENCE]" count = utopia_manifesto_paid_growth_unit_count owner = ROOT } }
				FORMATION_NAME = "[This.GetUtopiaManifestoMilitaryFormationName]"
				START_EXPERIENCE = "[?utopia_manifesto_paid_growth_experience]"
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_formation_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_military_formation.worker_defense } }
			meta_effect = {
				text = { create_unit = { division = "name = \"[FORMATION_NAME]\" division_template = \"[FORMATION_NAME]\" start_experience_factor = [START_EXPERIENCE]" count = utopia_manifesto_paid_growth_unit_count owner = ROOT } }
				FORMATION_NAME = "[This.GetUtopiaManifestoMilitaryFormationName]"
				START_EXPERIENCE = "[?utopia_manifesto_paid_growth_experience]"
			}
		}
		else_if = {

			meta_effect = {

utopia_manifesto_execute_paid_military_growth = {
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_negative_military_manpower_cost = utopia_manifesto_military_manpower_cost }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_negative_military_infantry_cost = utopia_manifesto_military_infantry_equipment_cost }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_negative_military_support_cost = utopia_manifesto_military_support_equipment_cost }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_negative_military_army_xp_cost = utopia_manifesto_military_army_experience_cost }
	multiply_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_negative_military_manpower_cost = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.negative_one }
	multiply_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_negative_military_infantry_cost = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.negative_one }
	multiply_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_negative_military_support_cost = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.negative_one }
	multiply_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_negative_military_army_xp_cost = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.negative_one }
	add_manpower = utopia_manifesto_negative_military_manpower_cost
	add_army_experience = utopia_manifesto_negative_military_army_xp_cost
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = utopia_manifesto_negative_military_infantry_cost }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = utopia_manifesto_negative_military_support_cost }
	utopia_manifesto_create_paid_formation_template = yes
	utopia_manifesto_deploy_paid_formation = yes
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_military_growth_batches } } set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_growth_batches = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero } }
	add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_growth_batches = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one }
	set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_military_growth_proven
}

utopia_manifesto_reset_military_growth_inputs = {
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_growth_tier_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_growth_limits.minimum_tier }
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_military_formation_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_military_formation.unset }
}

utopia_manifesto_apply_paid_military_growth = {
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_military_growth_payment_failed
	utopia_manifesto_prepare_military_formation_input = yes
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_dynamic_costs = yes
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_can_pay_military_growth = yes }
		utopia_manifesto_execute_paid_military_growth = yes
	}
	else = {
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_military_growth_payment_failed
	}
	utopia_manifesto_reset_military_growth_inputs = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_apply_paid_focus_military_growth = {
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_military_growth_payment_failed
	utopia_manifesto_prepare_military_formation_input = yes
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_dynamic_costs = yes
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_can_pay_military_growth = yes }
		utopia_manifesto_execute_paid_military_growth = yes
	}
	else = {
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_military_growth_payment_failed
	}
	utopia_manifesto_reset_military_growth_inputs = yes
}

utopia_manifesto_apply_paid_institutional_growth = {
	clr_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_institutional_growth_payment_failed
	utopia_manifesto_refresh_dynamic_costs = yes
	if = {
		limit = { utopia_manifesto_can_pay_institutional_growth = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_negative_institutional_manpower_cost = utopia_manifesto_institutional_manpower_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_negative_institutional_support_cost = utopia_manifesto_institutional_support_equipment_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_negative_institutional_political_power_cost = utopia_manifesto_institutional_political_power_cost }
		multiply_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_negative_institutional_manpower_cost = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.negative_one }
		multiply_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_negative_institutional_support_cost = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.negative_one }
		multiply_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_negative_institutional_political_power_cost = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.negative_one }
		add_manpower = utopia_manifesto_negative_institutional_manpower_cost
		add_political_power = utopia_manifesto_negative_institutional_political_power_cost
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = utopia_manifesto_negative_institutional_support_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_growth_limits.institutional_need_reduction_per_tier }
		multiply_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_delta = utopia_manifesto_growth_tier_input }
		multiply_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_need_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.negative_one }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_plenty_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_growth_limits.institutional_plenty_per_tier }
		multiply_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_plenty_delta = utopia_manifesto_growth_tier_input }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_growth_limits.institutional_concord_per_tier }
		multiply_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_concord_delta = utopia_manifesto_growth_tier_input }
		set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_assignment_delta = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero }
		utopia_manifesto_apply_prepared_ledger_delta = yes
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = utopia_manifesto_institutional_growth_batches } } set_variable = { utopia_manifesto_institutional_growth_batches = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.zero } }
		add_to_variable = { utopia_manifesto_institutional_growth_batches = constant:utopia_manifesto_math.one }
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_institutional_growth_proven
		utopia_manifesto_evaluate_active_evolutions = yes
	}
	else = {
		set_country_flag = utopia_manifesto_institutional_growth_payment_failed
	}
	set_temp_variable = { utopia_manifesto_growth_tier_input = constant:utopia_manifesto_growth_limits.minimum_tier }
}
